Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [101] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent World Recovery (LWR)框架，通过模态特定嵌入的潜在空间对齐和可用性感知融合，在不完整模态条件下实现鲁棒的多模态学习，并在多组学癌症表型分类和生存预测任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物科学应用中异质模态通常仅在部分可用，而现有方法依赖固定的模态集合或显式插补缺失模态，导致误差传播和实用受限，亟需一种能够处理任意可用模态组合的鲁棒多模态学习方法。

Method: LWR包含两个核心设计：(i) 基于邻居关系的潜在空间对齐，将不同模态的特定嵌入映射到共享潜在空间；(ii) 可用性感知融合，仅在训练和推理时使用实际可用的模态嵌入构建统一表示，而非插补缺失模态。

Result: 在真实世界的不完整多组学基准数据集上，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下流任务中取得了有效且鲁棒的结果。

Conclusion: 将缺失模态视为对潜在状态的部分感知，通过避免显式重构缺失模态来规避误差传播，为缺失模态下的多模态学习提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

</details>


### [102] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次复杂度架构，在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练等复杂任务上，xLSTM表现最优，其优势源于更灵活稳定的门控机制带来的状态跟踪与记忆修正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力计算成本高昂，次二次架构虽具可扩展性替代潜力，但哪种设计能构建最有效的序列模型尚不明确，亟需系统对比分析。

Method: 选取xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种代表性次二次架构，在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三类复杂依赖任务上进行评估，并提出统一公式化框架分析其状态跟踪与记忆动力学机制。

Result: xLSTM在所有设置下均取得最佳整体性能；其门控方案能实现更灵活稳定的记忆修正；在受控的合成长度泛化任务上验证了上述发现。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的性能优势源于其稳健的状态跟踪与累积能力，尤其是通过门控机制实现的灵活记忆修正。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [103] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bebop方法，系统研究多 token 预测（MTP）在大语言模型RL后训练中的应用。发现MTP接受率受模型熵波动限制，提出端到端TV损失函数优化拒绝采样接受率，结合预RL MTP训练策略，在数学推理、代码生成等任务上实现高达1.8x的异步RL训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是关键瓶颈，MTP虽可通过投机解码加速，但在RL训练中接受率显著下降导致加速有限，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 1) 揭示MTP接受率与模型熵的负线性关系；2) 提出概率拒绝采样替代贪心草稿采样以缓解熵扰动；3) 提出端到端全变差（TV）损失直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率；4) 验证预RL MTP训练策略可保持一致的接受率，避免在线更新开销。

Result: TV损失带来约10%接受率提升，最高达95%接受率；额外25%推理吞吐增益；在Qwen3.5/3.6/3.7模型上实现高达1.8x异步RL训练端到端加速。

Conclusion: 预RL阶段的MTP训练配合端到端TV损失和拒绝采样，可在整个RL训练过程中保持稳定加速效果，是集成MTP到大规模RL流水线的实用方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


### [104] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)，通过将分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元迁移到细粒度决策点，并在13个基准上取得约4个点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习方法在粗粒度启发式单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流）上进行信用分配，难以识别中间决策对下游结果的影响。

Method: APPO利用结合token不确定性和策略诱导似然增益的Branching Score选择分支位置，并引入过程级优势缩放以更好地在分支 rollout 间分配信用。

Result: 在13个基准上，APPO一致地提升了强智能体RL基线近4个点，同时保持高效的工具调用和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将有影响力的决策点广泛分布于生成序列中而非集中在工具调用处，令牌熵单独不足以可靠反映其对最终结果的影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

</details>


### [105] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS（主动理论学习的自动化科学框架），通过在强化学习行为实验设计中迭代生成可解释的稀疏神经网络假设并设计最优区分实验，在bandit任务中实现5-10倍于随机实验的样本效率提升，验证了该方法在认知科学中自动化发现机制模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知科学中，通过机制建模推进科学理解需要提出正确的实验问题以获取信息量最大的数据，然而当前依赖人工设计实验，效率低且难以自动化。作者旨在构建一个自动化闭环框架，将假设生成与实验设计协同优化，以加速可解释行为模型的发现。

Method: ATLAS框架包含两个核心迭代步骤：(1) 假设生成——通过一组多样化的稀疏神经网络（Disentangled RNNs）集成来表示机制性假设；(2) 实验设计——主动选择最优区分这些假设的实验。在bandit任务中，ATLAS生成具有时间结构的不同类型实验序列，并针对强化学习智能体的行为恢复进行测试。

Result: ATLAS在所有评估指标（行为、结构、计算相似度）上相较于随机实验实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升，其性能还通过与文献中专家设计的实验进行对比得到了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在认知科学及其他依赖机制模型发现的科学领域中，通过主动学习自动化加速可解释性洞察的潜力。

Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

</details>


### [106] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将MoE路由器的每一行与对应专家的主奇异方向对齐，并基于此设计了Manifold Power Iteration（MPI）方法，通过“Power-then-Retract”范式使路由权重收敛到专家的主奇异方向，从而提升MoE模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由器的每一行本应编码对应专家矩阵的表示向量，使点积能反映token与专家的亲和度，但缺乏设计原则来强制这种压缩对齐。

Method: 提出Manifold Power Iteration（MPI），采用“Power-then-Retract”范式：对路由权重进行幂迭代步骤，然后通过收缩操作施加范数约束，确保效率与稳定性，驱动路由行收敛到对应专家的主奇异方向。

Result: 理论证明MPI能使路由器行收敛到专家主奇异方向；实验在1B到11B参数的MoE模型上进行预训练，验证了这种对齐能带来更有效的MoE模型。

Conclusion: 将路由器行与专家主奇异方向对齐是一种有效的设计原则，MPI方法能实现这一对齐并提升MoE模型性能。

Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Introducing an Extensible Open-Source Toolkit Suite for Studying Second Harmonic Generation: A Case Study of Depleted Pulsed Gaussian Wave SHG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11228)
*Mostafa M. Rezaee,Mohammad Sabaeian,Alireza Motazedian,Fatemeh Sedaghat Jalil-Abadi,Mohammad Ghadri*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个针对二次谐波产生（SHG）中热效应的计算工具包套件，该套件包含多个独立但协调的建模工具，覆盖不同物理条件下的SHG场景，提供文档化的数值实现、可重复的工作流程和示例，以加速SHG研究并促进可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SHG模型依赖简化假设，热效应导致方程高度耦合且非线性，难以解析求解；实验上也无法获取晶体内部每点的时空温度数据。因此需要一套数值计算工具来弥补这一空白。

Method: 开发了SHG计算工具包套件，包含多个独立工具包，每个专注于特定配置或耦合机制，整体提供文档化的数值实现、可重复工作流程和示例。

Result: 形成了一个完整的基础设施，支持研究人员复制、适配和扩展方法，无需重复基础开发工作。

Conclusion: 该工具包套件加速了SHG研究并促进了可重复性，为热效应等复杂条件下的SHG计算研究提供了结构化解决方案。

Abstract: Second Harmonic Generation (SHG) in nonlinear crystals has been extensively investigated, but most existing models still rely on simplifying assumptions. In realistic settings, thermal effects introduce complications that are difficult to capture analytically because the governing equations are highly coupled and nonlinear. Direct experimental characterization is also limited, since studying thermal effects would require spatiotemporal temperature data at every point in the crystal, which is not experimentally accessible.
  To address these limitations, we have developed a SHG Computational Toolkit Suite, a coordinated collection of independent modeling toolkits that cover different SHG scenarios under various physical conditions. Each toolkit focuses on a particular configuration or coupling mechanism, while the suite as a whole provides well-documented numerical implementations, reproducible workflows, and illustrative examples.
  Together, this article and the Toolkit Suite provide a coherent infrastructure for computational studies of SHG. It enables researchers to replicate, adapt, and extend our methods without duplicating foundational development efforts, thereby accelerating SHG research and promoting reproducibility.

</details>


### [108] [Physically Constrained Ensemble Gaussian Process Modelling for Expensive Quantum Systems with Heteroskedastic Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11240)
*Arpan Biswas,Surtirtha Paul,Joseph Agada,Matthias Thamm,Adrian Del Maestro*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理约束集成高斯过程（pc-EGP）框架，用于在异方差噪声和稀疏采样条件下高效建模复杂量子多体系统，并通过物理一致性约束提升预测的准确性和物理合理性。在Bose-Hubbard模型的DMRG模拟和纳米孔硅酸盐中量子液体的QMC模拟两个案例中，pc-EGP相比传统GP取得了更好的精度与物理意义平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的精确建模（如DMRG、QMC）计算成本极高，限制了参数空间的广泛探索；且这些昂贵模拟在不同参数区域存在可变误差（异方差噪声），需要量化与传播。因此需要一种能够在稀疏、异方差噪声数据下准确估计函数空间并保持物理相关性的预测模型。

Method: 提出pc-EGP框架：1）将物理约束作为用户可控的加权惩罚项加入GP代理模型的数据驱动损失函数中；2）通过数值求积方法训练一组GP集成模型，在多个节点上的GP通过求积加权平均进行整合。

Result: 在合成数据验证后，应用于两个量子系统案例：1）基于DMRG模拟的Bose-Hubbard模型，预测超流-莫特绝缘体相变的临界相互作用参数Uc；2）基于QMC模拟的纳米孔硅酸盐中量子液体，优化化学环境以实现一维超流。pc-EGP相比传统GP在准确性和物理意义预测之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: pc-EGP能够有效建模含异方差噪声的稀疏量子系统数据，在保持物理一致性的同时提升预测性能，适用于计算昂贵的量子多体模拟场景。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of quantum many-body systems often requires computationally expensive simulations such as Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) or Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) calculations. These methods, while precise, impose significant time and resource constraints, limiting their use in exhaustive parameter exploration. Moreover, these expensive simulations can contain variable errors over the large unknown parameter space, which needs to be quantified and propagated. Thus, predictive modelling is required to estimate the functional space accurately over scarcely sampled data with heteroskedastic noise, while preserving the physical relevance of the estimation. Therefore, we present a Physically Constrained Ensemble Gaussian Process (pc-EGP) framework designed to efficiently model complex and noisy quantum systems under physical consistency constraints. The proposed method first enforces physical constraints as a user controlled weighted penalty to the data-driven loss function of the Gaussian Process (GP) surrogates. Then an ensemble of such GP models is trained with variable noisy simulations via numerical quadrature method where these multiple GP(s) at different nodes is integrated as a quadrature weighted average. We first demonstrate the framework on synthetically generated data before applying to quantum systems. In the first case study, we leverage DMRG simulations of the Bose-Hubbard Model to predict the critical interaction parameter Uc governing the superfluid-to-Mott-insulator transition. In the second case study, we demonstrate our method on QMC simulations, of a quantum liquid confined inside a nanoporous silicate with the goal of optimizing a chemical environment to realize a one-dimensional superfluid. Compared to conventional GP, pc-EGP achieves a better balance of accuracy and physically meaningful predictions.

</details>


### [109] [fitPALSpectra: Python fitting of positron annihilation lifetime spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12157)
*Georgios E. Pavlou*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了fitPALSpectra，一个开源的Python工作流，用于正电子湮没寿命谱（PALS）的模拟、拟合、可视化和报告。该工具使用解析积分后的指数-高斯响应模型，支持可配置的源和样品组分、约束优化、可选的最小二乘精炼，并输出拟合结果、相关矩阵和拟合曲线。在完全合成谱上的验证表明，该方法能准确恢复模拟的寿命、强度、探测器半高宽、时间偏移和背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PALS谱分析中多指数寿命模型拟合对初始参数选择、参数边界、源校正以及寿命与强度参数之间的相关性非常敏感，现有方法在实际应用中存在稳定性问题。

Method: 开发了fitPALSpectra开源Python工作流，采用解析积分的指数-高斯响应模型，包含可配置的源和样品组分、约束优化、可选的最小二乘精炼，并提供机器可读的输出格式。

Result: 在完全合成谱上的验证显示，该方法能够准确恢复模拟的寿命、强度、探测器半高宽、时间偏移和背景等参数。

Conclusion: fitPALSpectra提供了一种可配置、开源且稳定的PALS谱分析方案，有效解决了传统拟合方法对初始参数敏感的逆问题。

Abstract: Positron annihilation lifetime spectroscopy (PALS) spectra are commonly analyzed by fitting multi-exponential lifetime models convoluted with the detector resolution function. In practice, this inverse problem is sensitive to initial parameter choices, parameter bounds, source corrections, and correlations between lifetime and intensity parameters. This paper presents fitPALSpectra, an open-source Python workflow for configurable PALS spectrum simulation, fitting, visualization, and reporting. The implementation uses an analytically integrated exponential--Gaussian response model, configurable source and sample components, constrained optimization, optional least-squares refinement, and machine-readable output of fit results, correlation matrices, and fitted curves. Validation on fully synthetic spectra with known ground-truth parameters shows accurate recovery of the simulated lifetimes, intensities, detector full width at half maximum, prompt shift, and background.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Exact Entanglement Dynamics Beyond Nearest-Neighbor Dual-Unitary Floquet Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11311)
*Tanay Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一类有限范围交错踢变伊辛模型，通过在子格上保持对偶幺正性并允许子格间耦合，推导出精确的闭合形式纠缠动力学，突破了传统仅限最近邻对偶幺正模型的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶幺正性在最近邻结构中可获得精确结果，但有限范围相互作用通常导致复杂性。作者希望超越最近邻框架，构建一族解析可解的有限范围模型，以研究更一般的纠缠增长行为。

Method: 提出一种交错结构，在子格上保持对偶幺正性，然后通过子格间耦合构建有限范围踢变伊辛模型。对于最小相互作用范围 r=2，推导出所有 n-Rényi 纠缠熵在所有时间点的精确表达式，并证明结果为两个耦合子格贡献之和。该框架可自然推广到更大相互作用范围和异质局部希尔伯特空间。

Result: 对于 r=2 的有限范围模型，得到了所有 n-Rényi 纠缠熵的精确闭式解，结果显示为两个耦合子格贡献的线性叠加。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个超越严格最近邻对偶幺正模型的可控解析框架，可用于研究精确纠缠增长行为，并能自然推广至更大范围和异质系统。

Abstract: Exact results using dual-unitarity largely rely on nearest-neighbor structures, while finite-range interactions typically lead to complications. Going beyond the usual nearest-neighbor setting, we introduce an analytically tractable family of finite-range kicked Ising models that admit exact closed-form entanglement dynamics. The construction is based on a staggered structure in which dual-unitarity is present on sublattices that are then coupled to each other. The central observation is that these inter-sublattice couplings do not obstruct the dual-unitarity of the resulting model. For the minimal interaction range of $r= 2$, we derive exact expressions for all the $n-$Rényi entanglement entropies at all times and show that the result is the sum of the two coupled sublattice contributions. Our framework extends naturally to larger finite interaction ranges and to systems with heterogeneous local Hilbert spaces, without additional assumptions. It thus provides a controlled setting for studying exact entanglement growth beyond strictly nearest-neighbor dual-unitary models.

</details>


### [111] [Observable signatures of exceptional points from left-right eigenstate distinction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11333)
*Leela Ganesh Chandra Lakkaraju,Soumik Bandyopadhyay,Sudipto Singha Roy,Philipp Hauke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一维复XY自旋链模型，发展了基于左右本征向量区分来检测非厄米系统中奇异点（Exceptional Points）的理论框架，并提出了静态局域自旋关联和动力学淬火后时间平均纠缠熵等可观测特征作为检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统在量子传感、单向输运和鲁棒激光等领域具有重要应用，而奇异点（Exceptional Points）是其中关键的谱奇异特征。然而，如何在多体系统中通过可观测量有效识别奇异点仍是一个重要问题。

Method: 以一维复XY自旋链为模型，分别实现RT对称和PT对称区域。通过构造哈密顿量与其厄米共轭之间的全局差异量定位奇异点；利用左右本征态上的局域自旋关联差异作为静态检测方案；通过突然淬火后的时间平均左右纠缠熵差异编码奇异点特征。

Result: 全局差异量通过非解析行为定位奇异点；静态左右本征态局域自旋关联差异可可靠检测奇异点，而静态二分纠缠熵则无法区分；在RT对称区域，时间平均左右纠缠熵差在奇异点处呈现显著峰值；在PT对称区域，该量表现为序参量，在一相有限、在相变点消失。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了非厄米本征态结构与奇异点可观测特征之间的直接联系，为在现有量子模拟器中识别奇异点提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian quantum systems exhibit qualitatively distinct physical behavior compared to Hermitian systems, a prime example being spectral singularities known as exceptional points. Their relevance in, e.g., quantum sensing, unidirectional transport, and robust lasing makes it important to be able to identify exceptional points through observable features of a many-body system. Here, using as an example a one-dimensional complex XY spin chain realizing both rotation-time RT- and parity-time PT-symmetric regimes, we develop a framework for detecting exceptional points based on the distinction between left and right eigenvectors of the Hamiltonian, which in a non-Hermitian system are no longer the adjoint of each other. We first show that a global measure constructed from the difference between the Hamiltonian and its adjoint locates exceptional points via distinct non-analytic behavior. At the level of observables, differences in local spin correlations evaluated on the right and left eigenstates provide a reliable static detection scheme. In contrast, static bipartite entanglement measures fail to capture this distinction, urging us to study the quantum dynamics of the model. Following a sudden quench, we demonstrate that the time-averaged right-left entanglement entropy difference directly encodes signatures of the exceptional point. In the RT-symmetric regime, it exhibits a pronounced peak at the exceptional point, whereas in the PT-symmetric regime it behaves as an order-parameter-like quantity, remaining finite in one phase and vanishing at the transition. Our results establish a direct link between the structure of non-Hermitian eigenstates and observable signatures of exceptional points, providing a practical route to identify them in existing quantum simulators.

</details>


### [112] [Q-DICE: Quantum Distributed Interconnect Compiler and Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11340)
*Michael Silver,Zachary Vernec,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DICE，一个面向分布式量子计算的硬件感知仿真环境，支持在经典模拟器和NISQ时代单体硬件上基准测试分布式量子电路。核心贡献包括：分布式QPU后端的程序化构建方案（QPU切片与拼接技术）、基于物理驱动Kraus算子的非局域链路噪声建模方法、以及边界感知的电路映射算法。在包括分布式Grover搜索在内的实验中，仿真与实验结果的最坏保真度偏差仅为4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算有望实现可扩展量子计算，但缺乏在真实物理条件下评估分布式算法的工具，研究人员无法对分布式协议进行有效基准测试。

Method: (1) 提出QPU切片与拼接技术，用于构建分布式QPU后端；(2) 利用物理驱动的Kraus算子和随机误差信道建模非局域链路噪声；(3) 设计边界感知电路映射算法，在编译过程中强制执行分布式QPU拓扑约束。

Result: 在多种实验验证的量子电路上，包括基于光链路的捕获离子硬件上的分布式Grover搜索，仿真与实验结果的最坏保真度偏差仅为4%。

Conclusion: Q-DICE能够准确复现真实分布式量子系统的行为，为分布式量子算法和架构的实验研究提供了高效的工具支持。

Abstract: As distributed quantum computing (DQC) offers a leading path towards scalable quantum computation, the ability to benchmark distributed algorithms under realistic conditions becomes critical for system co-design. However, without access to physical systems, researchers lack tools to evaluate distribution protocols. We introduce Q-DICE (Quantum Distributed Interconnect Compiler and Emulator), a hardware-aware emulation environment for benchmarking distributed quantum circuits on classical simulators and on NISQ-era monolithic hardware. This work provides three core contributions: (1) a programmatic scheme to construct distributed QPU backends, utilizing two novel techniques - QPU slicing and stitching - to facilitate distributed circuit mapping, (2) a methodology for modeling nonlocal link noise using physically motivated Kraus operators and stochastic error channels, and (3) a boundary-aware circuit mapping algorithm enforcing distributed QPU topology constraints during transpilation. Together, these components constitute a distribution-aware compiler and noise-modeling engine that faithfully enforces the physical limitations of distributed quantum hardware within existing execution environments. We validate Q-DICE against a multitude of experimentally demonstrated quantum circuits, including a distributed Grover's search on optically linked trapped-ion hardware, achieving a worst-case fidelity deviation of 4% between simulated and experimental results. These findings demonstrate Q-DICE's capacity to accurately reproduce real distributed quantum system behavior across platforms, streamlining experimentation with distributed quantum algorithms and architectures.

</details>


### [113] [Fisher geometry reshapes the effect of incompatibility in multiparameter quantum estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11343)
*Jiayu He,Matteo G. A. Paris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多参数量子估计中，不相容性在Fisher信息矩阵本征基上的分布（而非总量）才是决定精度权衡代价的关键，并提出了量化匹配度的无量纲量$G_n^{(F)}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子估计面临"sloppiness"（QFIM各向异性导致部分参数方向不敏感）和"incompatibility"（不同参数的最优测量不对易）两大障碍。虽然权衡界$C_T$捕捉了二者的联合影响，但不兼容性在不同参数平面上的分布如何影响总体代价这一问题尚不清楚。

Method: 引入无量纲量$G_n^{(F)}$量化不相容性分布与QFIM特征向量之间的对齐程度；通过因式分解展示不相容性贡献的Frobenius标度；推导出不相容性代价的理论界及其与秩相关的倍数关系。

Result: 证明了在固定sloppiness（即固定Fisher体积）时，将不相容性集中到单个参数平面可以降低优化后的权衡代价，因为Fisher几何可被重塑以分配更多Fisher面积到该平面。qutrit $SU(2)$编码的数值实验表明，若匹配因子$G$足够小，即使不相容性强度更大的态也能产生更小的代价。

Conclusion: 不相容性相对于Fisher本征基的分布是多参数估计的核心诊断量，其重要性超越不相容性的总强度。

Abstract: Multiparameter quantum estimation faces two fundamental obstacles: sloppiness, i.e., anisotropy of the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) that renders some parameter directions insensitive, and incompatibility, the non-commutativity of optimal measurements for different parameters. The trade-off bound $C_T$ captures their joint impact on precision, but it has remained unclear how the distribution of incompatibility across parameter planes affects its overall cost. Here we separate the total amount of incompatibility from its location. We introduce a dimensionless quantity $G_n^{(F)}$ that measures the alignment between the incompatibility distribution and the eigenvalues of the QFIM, and show how the Frobenius scale of the incompatibility contribution factorizes. We obtain a bound and prove the incompatibility cost lies between this bound and a rank-dependent multiple thereof. We also prove that at fixed sloppiness, or equivalently fixed Fisher volume, concentrating incompatibility into a single parameter plane reduces the optimized trade-off cost because the Fisher geometry can then be reshaped to allocate more Fisher area to that plane. A qutrit $SU(2)$ encoding numerically confirms that states with larger incompatibility strength can nevertheless incur a smaller cost if the matching factor $G$ is sufficiently small. Our results establish that the distribution of incompatibility relative to the Fisher eigenbasis is a central diagnostic for multiparameter estimation, beyond the total incompatibility strength.

</details>


### [114] [Rolling Stock Planning Using the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11383)
*Jiří Guth Jarkovský,Patricia Bickert,Elisabeth Wybo,Martin Leib*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将铁路调度中的车辆规划问题转化为最大权重独立集（MWIS）问题，并提出一种混合分治算法，结合经典精确方法和量子近似优化算法（QAOA）来求解，实验表明增大子图规模可提升解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车辆规划是一个复杂的组合优化问题，需在满足维护等约束条件下为190个行程分配列车并最小化运营成本，传统方法难以高效处理大规模搜索空间。

Method: 将问题建模为图上的MWIS问题，节点表示可行列车循环；提出混合分治算法，迭代选择子图并分别用经典精确方法和QAOA求解；QAOA同时通过经典模拟和IQM Emerald量子设备执行。

Result: 增大子图规模通常能提升解的质量；混合框架有效桥接了多项式时间近似求解器与指数时间精确方法之间的差距。

Conclusion: 混合分治框架结合QAOA在解决大规模铁路车辆规划问题上具有潜力，量子方法在子图规模扩展时表现出改善效果。

Abstract: Rolling stock planning is a complex optimization problem in railway management that involves assigning physical trains to scheduled trips while minimizing operational costs. In this work, we address a specific instance of this problem featuring 190 trips over two days, subject to constraints such as mandatory maintenance stops. We reformulate the problem as a Maximum-Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem on a graph where nodes represent feasible train cycles. To handle the computational complexity of the large search space, we propose a hybrid divide-and-conquer algorithm. This approach iteratively selects subgraphs and solves the MWIS problem using various solvers, including exact classical methods and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). We evaluate the algorithm's performance by comparing these methods and analyzing the scaling with respect to subgraph size, with QAOA assessed through both classical simulation and execution on a quantum device (IQM Emerald). Our results indicate that increasing the subgraph size generally improves solution quality, demonstrating that the hybrid framework can effectively bridge the gap between polynomial-time approximate solvers and exponential-time exact methods.

</details>


### [115] [Isotropic random walks and Brownian diffusion on complex projective space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11438)
*Gyula I. Tóth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了复射影空间上各向同性随机游走的解析框架，用于探索量子态空间，给出了转移核、保真度统计和Fubini-Study度规下几何可观测量的显式解析表达式，并推导了首达时间问题的闭式及渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子态空间探索提供一个规范且解析可解的随机几何框架，绕开微观Lindblad生成元的指定，直接建立从随机纯态演化到Haar均衡与保真度统计的桥梁。

Method: 结合紧致秩一对称空间上的调和分析与随机纯态演化，利用Fubini-Study几何结构，推导转移核、保真度统计和几何观测量的显式解析表达式；在短时和长时极限下分别分析Brownian扩散行为和测度集中现象；对首达时间问题给出闭式解和渐近分析。

Result: 得到了各向同性随机游走的完整解析解，包括短时Brownian扩散（Fubini-Study Laplace-Beltrami算子生成）、长时Haar均衡及测度集中行为；给出了首达时间的闭式表达式（Brownian极限）及其长时尾分布，揭示了高保真度目标态下首达时间随维度强烈发散的特性。

Conclusion: 该框架为各向同性退极化与Haar均衡提供了一个可解参考模型，无需指定微观Lindblad生成元即可再现关键量子统计特征，并为高维量子态空间的随机几何分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We show that isotropic random walks on the complex projective space provide a canonical and analytically tractable stochastic-geometric framework for the exploration of quantum-state space. The approach combines harmonic analysis on compact rank-one symmetric spaces with stochastic pure-state evolution and yields explicit analytical expressions for transition kernels, fidelity statistics, and geometric observables associated with the Fubini--Study metric. In particular, the framework provides a solvable reference model for isotropic depolarization and Haar equilibration, reproducing Haar-random fidelity statistics and the invariant measure on projective Hilbert space without specifying a microscopic Lindblad generator. In the short-time regime, the stochastic evolution converges to Brownian diffusion generated by the Fubini--Study Laplace--Beltrami operator, while the long-time limit exhibits concentration-of-measure behaviour characteristic of high-dimensional random quantum states. We further derive analytical and asymptotic results for the first-passage-time problem, including closed-form expressions in the Brownian limit for the mean first passage time and the long-time tail of the first-passage-time distribution. For high-fidelity target states, the mean first passage time exhibits a strong dimension-dependent divergence originating from the concentration properties of the Fubini--Study geometry.

</details>


### [116] [Planted-Solution Pauli Hamiltonians as a Quantum Benchmarking Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11455)
*Amir Kalev,Itay Hen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造具有已知基态能量的泡利哈密顿量的方法，用于作为基态能量估计算法的参考实例。该构造将 planted block-product state 嵌入为无受挫局域子句之和的基态，仅以多项式规模的泡利算子线性组合形式呈现，并允许保持谱的克利福德共轭操作。该框架涵盖经典 planted 约束满足问题作为对角特例，提供直接的嵌入通道以继承经典硬度性质。同时公开了开源软件、认证密钥和示例实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基态能量估计算法提供具有确切已知基态能量的参考实例，便于算法性能的验证与比较。

Method: 通过将 planted block-product state 嵌入为无受挫局域子句之和的基态，构造仅以多项式大小泡利算子线性组合表示的哈密顿量，并支持保持谱的克利福德共轭变换。

Result: 成功构造了一类具有已知基态能量的泡利哈密顿量，该构造可继承经典 planted 约束满足问题的硬度特性，并提供了公开可用的软件和实例。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子基态能量估计算法提供了可控的基准测试实例，且通过经典硬问题的嵌入通道，有望用于验证量子优势。

Abstract: We introduce a construction of Pauli Hamiltonians with exactly known ground-state energies, intended as reference instances for ground-state energy estimation algorithms. The construction embeds a planted block-product state as the simultaneous ground state of a sum of frustration-free local clauses on overlapping supports, exposes the resulting model only as a polynomial-size linear combination of Pauli operators, and admits optional Clifford conjugation that preserves the spectrum. The framework subsumes classical planted constraint-satisfaction problems as a diagonal special case, providing a direct embedding channel through which classical hardness properties can be inherited. Open-source software, certification keys, and example instances are made publicly available.

</details>


### [117] [Optimizing Encoder Circuits of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum LDPC Codes via Beam Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11468)
*Aditya Sodhani,Pavan Kumar,Shayan Srinivasa Garani,Keshab K. Parhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于波束搜索的编码器优化方法，通过GF(2)行操作分解CNOT子序列，在EA量子QC-LDPC码上实现7.3-34.0%的CNOT门数缩减，并优于Patel-Markov-Hayes综合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EA量子QC-LDPC码的实际应用依赖于高效的编码器电路和预共享纠缠对(ebits)，而基于稳定子形式主义的编码器实现中，受控门是复杂度的主要贡献因素。现有SKG编码器构建方法仍有优化空间。

Method: 采用SKG编码器构建，将编码器优化转化为GF(2)上的行操作搜索问题，以分解源自CNOT子序列的二进制矩阵。使用基于汉明距离启发式的波束搜索算法求解该优化问题。

Result: 在测试的EA量子QC-LDPC码族上，该方法相比SKG基线编码器实现了7.3-34.0%的CNOT门数缩减，且在所有测试实例中优化后的电路CNOT门数均低于Patel-Markov-Hayes综合方法。

Conclusion: 对于结构化的EA QC-LDPC码，可以实现显著的编码器简化，优化后的电路已通过稳定子表格仿真验证。

Abstract: Entanglement-assisted (EA) quantum QC-LDPC codes offer strong error-correction capabilities with structured parity-check matrices, but their practical use depends on efficient encoder circuits and the availability of pre-shared Bell pairs (ebits). In all encoder implementations based on the stabilizer formalism, the dominant contribution to this complexity comes from the use of controlled gates. In this paper, we adopt the Sharma-Kumar-Garani (SKG) encoder construction. We formulate the encoder optimization as a search over GF(2) row operations that decompose the binary matrix derived from its CNOT sub-sequence. We solve this problem using a beam search algorithm guided by a Hamming-distance heuristic. For the tested EA quantum QC-LDPC code families, the proposed method achieves CNOT-count reductions of 7.3-34.0% relative to the SKG baseline encoder. The optimized circuits also yield lower CNOT counts than Patel-Markov-Hayes synthesis on all tested instances and are verified by stabilizer-tableau simulation. These results show that substantial encoder simplification is possible for structured EA QC-LDPC codes.

</details>


### [118] [Linear Combination of Hamiltonian Simulation with Commutator Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11475)
*Junaid Aftab,Dong An,Konstantina Trivisa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在LCHS框架下，通过引入多乘积公式（MPF）实现哈密顿量模拟步骤，得到了对易子敏感的误差与复杂度边界，并分析了不同求积规则对离散误差、对易子结构及查询复杂度的影响，比较了均匀梯形和自由尺度sinh-sinh求积法的性能，展示了在分数扩散、对流-扩散和开放量子系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LCHS分析基于耗散生成元的范数量实现了时间和精度上的近最优缩放，但忽略了哈密顿量模拟步骤中对易子结构对误差和复杂度的影响，本文旨在通过引入MPF来填补这一空白。

Method: 在LCHS框架中使用多乘积公式（MPF）实现哈密顿量模拟，对抽象MPF误差分布和一般时间无关及局域哈密顿量进行求积后分析，使用已知的对易子敏感的MPF误差估计，并比较均匀梯形求积与自由尺度sinh-sinh求积两种方法。

Result: 证明了求积规则不仅影响离散误差，还影响对易子结构和查询复杂度；自由尺度sinh-sinh求积相比均匀梯形求积在求积基数缩放上表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过MPF实现的LCHS框架能够获得对易子敏感的误差与复杂度边界，选择合适的求积规则可以提升性能，该框架在分数扩散、对流-扩散和开放量子系统等应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: The Linear Combination of Hamiltonian Simulation (LCHS) framework simulates dissipative linear dynamics by representing time evolution as an integral over unitary operators, which is discretized by quadrature and implemented via Hamiltonian simulation. While existing analyses achieve near-optimal scaling in time and precision using norm-based quantities of the dissipative generator, we show that implementing the Hamiltonian simulation steps with Multi-Product Formulas (MPFs) yields commutator-sensitive error and complexity bounds. We demonstrate that the quadrature rule affects not only discretization error but also commutator structure and query complexity. This dependence is quantified through post-quadrature analysis for abstract MPF error profiles and for general time-independent and local Hamiltonians using known commutator-sensitive MPF error estimates. We compare uniform trapezoidal and free-scale sinh--sinh quadrature, showing improved quadrature-cardinality scaling for the latter, and illustrate the framework with applications to fractional diffusion, advection--diffusion, and open quantum systems.

</details>


### [119] [PHASE: Pauli Hierarchical Assembly on Subdivided Elements for Quantum-Compatible Operator Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11478)
*Tillman Philo,Caglar Oskay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出PHASE算法，利用分层递归网格划分实现有限元刚度矩阵的几何感知Pauli分解，将渐进复杂度从Θ(8^⌈log₂N⌉)降低到2^{γ_d⌈log₂N⌉}（γ_d<2），大幅提升大规模有限元模型的量子兼容算子合成可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限元刚度矩阵高效分解为Pauli基具有挑战性，因为Pauli字符串数量随问题规模指数增长。现有方法利用代数稀疏性或算子结构，但未能融入有限元离散化固有的几何组织，导致刚度矩阵的扩展性能较差。

Method: PHASE算法采用分层递归网格划分，在多个空间尺度上组织单元贡献；结合全空间和降空间张量化Pauli分解与基于快速Walsh-Hadamard变换的聚合策略，高效组装全局Pauli系数。

Result: 在标准网格正则性和平衡划分假设下，将Pauli组装渐进复杂度从2^{2⌈log₂N⌉}降低到2^{γ_d⌈log₂N⌉}，其中指数γ_d<2，实现了维度依赖的指数缩放指数缩减。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提升了大规模有限元模型的量子兼容算子合成可行性，为量子计算与有限元分析的交叉领域提供了高效的算子分解方案。

Abstract: Efficiently decomposing finite element stiffness matrices into the Pauli basis is challenging due to the exponential growth of Pauli strings with problem size. A naive Pauli expansion requires $Θ(8^{\lceil \log_2 N \rceil})$ operations, where $N$ denotes the number of degrees of freedom, rendering direct decomposition infeasible for large systems. Existing approaches exploit algebraic sparsity or operator structure but do not incorporate the geometric organization intrinsic to finite element discretizations, and consequently exhibit poor scaling for stiffness matrices. To address this problem, we introduce PHASE, a hierarchical, geometry-aware Pauli decomposition algorithm that leverages recursive mesh partitioning to organize element contributions across multiple spatial scales. PHASE employs a hybrid strategy that combines full- and reduced-space Tensorized Pauli Decomposition with Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform-based aggregation to assemble global Pauli coefficients efficiently. We show that this approach yields a dimension-dependent reduction in the exponential scaling exponent of Pauli assembly asymptotic complexity relative to existing methods, reducing the cost from $2^{2{\lceil \log_2 N \rceil}}$ to $2^{γ_d{\lceil \log_2 N \rceil}}$ with $γ_d < 2$ under standard mesh regularity and balanced partition assumptions. These results substantially improve the feasibility of quantum-compatible operator synthesis for large-scale finite element models.

</details>


### [120] [Handbook of Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11484)
*Victor V. Albert,Philippe Faist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了《错误纠正动物园》手册版，这是一个关于保护经典或量子信息免受存储和传输错误的方法的 curated 参考文献，包含错误纠正码的描述、符号分类、码间关系以及与其他数学物理对象（如球堆积、格点、设计、群、物质相）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误纠正技术广泛应用于条码扫描、通信、数据存储、卫星通信和量子计算等领域，需要一个系统化的参考来整理和分类各种错误纠正码及其相互关系。

Method: 构建了一个 curated 参考手册，对错误纠正码进行描述，按符号进行分类，并编录码之间以及码与球堆积、格点、设计、群、经典和量子物质相等相关对象的关系。

Result: 生成了一个既可作为严谨参考又可作为实用工具的手册，帮助追踪代码关系网络并发现新的连接。

Conclusion: 该手册旨在为错误纠正领域提供系统化的知识图谱，服务于从经典信息到量子信息的多种应用场景。

Abstract: Barcode scans, clear phone calls, reliable data storage, satellite communication, and large-scale quantum computation are all made possible by error correction. We present a handbook version of The Error Correction Zoo, a curated reference of methods for protecting classical or quantum information from errors during storage and transmission. The handbook includes descriptions of these error-correcting codes and a classification according to the symbols they use. It also catalogues relations among codes and related objects such as sphere packings, lattices, designs, groups, and classical and quantum phases of matter. The collection is intended both as a rigorous reference and as a practical aid for tracing the web of code relationships and uncovering new connections.

</details>


### [121] [A Cryogenic Uniaxial Strain Cell for Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11485)
*Bradley Lloyd,Davis Rash,Chandler Wilburn,Paul Kliewer,Meenakshi Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为厚方形半导体芯片设计的压电单轴应变单元，采用对称双芯片加载结构抑制弯曲变形和剪切应力，集成高密度RF/DC转接板和法拉第笼屏蔽，通过实验验证了在200μm厚硅片上施加215 με应变的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压电应变单元针对脆弱的高长比单晶优化，不适用于厚方形芯片的半导体量子器件，且需要在高电压压电致动器与密集RF/DC布线之间保持严格电隔离。

Method: 采用高度对称的双芯片加载配置来抑制弯曲变形和剪切应力；集成高密度RF/DC转接板以支持标准引线键合；将致动器封装在接地法拉第笼中以防止斯塔克位移；通过有限元仿真验证应变均匀性。

Result: 有限元仿真确认对称安装显著改善应变均匀性；实验验证在200V偏压下对200μm厚硅片施加了215 με的单轴应变。

Conclusion: 该应变单元成功实现了对厚方形半导体芯片的均匀单轴应变加载，并兼容量子比特所需的密集电学布线，为半导体量子器件的应变调控提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Mechanical strain is a powerful resource for tuning quantum systems, but existing piezoelectric strain cells are generally optimized for fragile, high-aspect-ratio single crystals rather than the thick, square-profile chips typical of semiconductor quantum devices. Furthermore, adapting these cells for qubits requires accommodating dense RF and DC wiring while maintaining strict electrical isolation from high-voltage piezo actuators. Here, we present a piezoelectric uniaxial strain cell designed to homogeneously strain thick, square-profile substrates. We introduce a highly symmetric dual-chip loading configuration that effectively suppresses flexural deformation and shear stress. The cell integrates a high-density RF/DC interposer to support standard wire bonding and encloses the actuators in a grounded Faraday cage to prevent unwanted Stark shifts in the device layer. Finite element simulations confirm that combining stiff actuators with this symmetric mounting drastically improves strain homogeneity. Finally, we validate the apparatus experimentally by applying uniaxial strain to a 200 $μ$m thick silicon die. Surface strain measurements demonstrate an applied strain of 215 $με$ for 200 V applied piezo bias.

</details>


### [122] [Logical error estimation from syndrome data of surface-code experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11496)
*Evangelia Takou,Cesar Benito,Arian Vezvaee,Daniel A. Lidar,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出从实验症状中估计量子纠错解码器误差模型（DEM）事件概率的方法，无需独立设备基准测试，并在Google Willow芯片和IBM ibm_miami处理器的表面码实验中验证了该方法能有效降低逻辑错误概率（通常5%-10%，部分IBM案例提升更大）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子纠错解码器依赖检测器误差模型（DEM），而DEM事件概率通常需要通过独立的设备基准测试获得，这不仅增加了实验负担，且可能引入额外误差。本文旨在探索是否能直接从实验症状数据中估计DEM概率，从而简化流程并提高解码性能。

Method: 从实验采集的量子纠错症状数据中直接估计DEM中各错误事件的概率，然后将其作为解码器的先验信息。该方法无需额外的校准电路、解码器微调或有监督拟合到逻辑结果。

Result: 在Google Willow芯片的公开表面码记忆实验数据和IBM ibm_miami处理器的模拟实验中，使用估计DEM相比基线设备信息DEM，通常能将逻辑错误概率降低5%-10%，部分IBM案例获得更大提升。

Conclusion: 从实验症状中直接估计DEM事件概率是可行的，且能产生有用的解码器先验，有助于降低译码后的逻辑错误概率。该方法在不同物理误差规模的设备上均有效，简化了量子纠错实验的校准流程。

Abstract: Decoders for quantum error correction (QEC) experiments rely on detector error models (DEMs), which encode, for each error, its probability and the detectors and logical observables it flips. Here we show that estimating DEM event probabilities from experimental syndromes is feasible, avoids independent device benchmarking, and produces useful decoder priors for estimating and reducing decoded logical error probabilities. We evaluate our methods using open-source data from surface-code memory experiments performed on Google's Willow chip, and we carry out analogous surface-code experiments on IBM's \texttt{ibm\_miami} processor. Despite the different physical error scales of the Google and IBM devices, in both cases our estimated DEMs improve logical error probabilities relative to baseline device-informed DEMs, typically at the $5\%-10\%$ level and with larger gains in some IBM cases, without additional calibration circuits, decoder fine-tuning, or supervised fitting to logical outcomes.

</details>


### [123] [Single Photon Cross-Phase Shifts Can Be Enhanced by Localization in both Frequency and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11516)
*Xinyu Jiao,Vida-Michelle Nixon,Kyle Thompson,Aephraim Steinberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验证明，通过后选择(post-selection)可以同时实现窄带宽和高峰值强度，打破单光子非线性中时间-能量不确定关系的限制，实验测得交叉相位移动增强6倍，与弱值理论定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子光学非线性面临根本性权衡：最大非线性需要同时满足频谱共振（窄带宽）和高峰值强度（短持续时间），但时间-能量不确定关系使这两个约束不相容。本文旨在探索后选择机制是否能打破这一限制。

Method: 实验上使用窄带共振光子源，先将其通过冷原子云透射，再通过探测实现时间上的局域化（后选择）。测量后选择态产生的交叉相位移动（XPS），并与无后选择的高斯单光子脉冲进行比较。

Result: 在光学深度OD=2.4±0.1时，后选择态的峰值XPS增强了6±1倍。实验结果与Thompson等人最近发展的原子激发弱值理论在多个光学深度下定性一致。

Conclusion: 后选择可以同时利用窄带宽制备态和高峰值强度后选择态的优势，突破单光子非线性中的时间-能量不确定关系限制。这一发现揭示了同时拥有频率和时间知识的新后果，引发了关于粒子在制备和后选择不对易时如何行为及与其他系统相互作用的基本问题。

Abstract: Single-photon optical nonlinearities face a fundamental trade-off: maximum nonlinearity requires both spectral resonance (narrow bandwidth) and high peak intensity (short duration), constraints that are incompatible due to the time-energy uncertainty relation. We demonstrate experimentally that this limitation does not need to exist in cases involving post-selection. We measure a cross-phase shift (XPS) produced by a resonant photon from a narrow-band source that is first transmitted through a cold atomic cloud and then localized in time through detection. The peak size of this XPS is greatly enhanced compared to that of Gaussian single-photon-level pulses without post-selection, benefiting from the narrow bandwidth of the resonant prepared state and the high intensity of the post-selected state simultaneously. We measure enhancements in the peak XPS of 6$\pm$1 at an optical depth (OD) of 2.4$\pm$0.1, and our results are in qualitative agreement across a range of optical depths with the recently developed weak value theory of atomic excitation [Thompson et al., APL Quantum 2, 036108 (2025)] for such post-selected photons. This work uncovers new consequences of having simultaneous knowledge of frequency and time, raising new foundational questions about how a particle behaves, and interacts with other systems, when its preparation and post-selection are non-commuting.

</details>


### [124] [Mach's principle in atomic transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11608)
*Subhajit Barman,Bibhas Ranjan Majhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了原子-镜面系统中圆周运动对原子跃迁概率的影响，发现两种场景（原子运动/镜面静止 vs 原子静止/镜面旋转）下跃迁概率具有结构相似性——交换场频率后结果等价，并将其解释为类似于经典马赫原理的半经典现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究圆周运动下原子-镜面系统中的原子跃迁概率特性，验证不同参考系下物理现象的对称性关联。

Method: 对两种场景进行理论建模与计算：1) 原子在静态圆柱镜面内做圆周运动；2) 圆柱镜面绕中心轴旋转而原子静止。比较两种场景下的原子跃迁概率结构。

Result: 两种场景下的原子跃迁概率具有结构相似性，在交换场频率后跃迁概率完全等价。

Conclusion: 这种等价性可被理解为一种半经典现象，类似于经典马赫原理中关于运动相对性的思想。

Abstract: We investigate the atomic transition probabilities in atom-mirror set-ups that are in circular motion. In one scenario, the atom is in circular motion inside a static cylindrical mirror. In the other scenario, the cylindrical mirror rotates around its central axis while the atom remains static. We report structural similarity in the atomic transition probabilities between these two cases -- these probabilities are equivalent upon interchanging the field frequencies between the two scenarios. We interpret such an observation as a semi-classical phenomenon analogous to the classical Mach's principle.

</details>


### [125] [Locally Acting Grover Mixers for Constraint-Preserving QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11530)
*Minjin Choi,Dongkeun Lee,Junghee Ryu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种局部作用的Grover混合器，用于量子交替算子拟设（GM-QAOA），通过在不相干的量子比特子系统上应用局部操作替代全局多控制相移门，降低电路实现开销。在精确覆盖问题和旅行商问题上的数值模拟表明，该方法在保持与原始GM-QAOA相当的收敛性能的同时，使用更浅的电路和更少的门，且对TSP问题仅编码部分约束到初始态能获得更紧凑的电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GM-QAOA中全局多控制相移门需要作用于所有量子比特，在近期量子设备上造成显著的电路开销，限制了其可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出局部作用的Grover混合器，针对具有不相干量子比特子系统乘积结构的初始态，用局部操作替代全局多控制相移门，保留初始态定义的搜索空间同时降低实现成本。

Result: 在精确覆盖问题和TSP上的数值模拟表明，所提方法在收敛行为上与原始GM-QAOA相当，但使用更浅的电路和更少的门。针对TSP，仅编码部分约束到初始态的策略结合所提混合器在达到可比解质量时电路更紧凑。

Conclusion: 局部Grover混合器能有效降低GM-QAOA的电路实现复杂度，有望提升其在近期量子设备上的可行性和扩展性。

Abstract: The Grover mixer quantum alternating operator ansatz (GM-QAOA) employs the Grover mixer to confine the quantum evolution to the feasible subspace defined by the problem. Its mixing unitary, however, requires a global multi-controlled phase-shift gate acting on all qubits, resulting in substantial circuit overhead on near-term quantum devices. In this work, we propose locally acting Grover mixers tailored to initial states that admit a product structure over disjoint qubit subsystems, which may be obtained by encoding only a subset of problem constraints into the initial state preparation. The proposed method preserves the search space defined by the initial state while significantly lowering implementation cost, as the global multi-controlled phase-shift gate is replaced with local operations on disjoint subsystems. Numerical simulations on the exact-cover problem and the traveling salesman problem (TSP) demonstrate that the proposed method achieves convergence behavior comparable to that of the original GM-QAOA, while using shallower circuits with fewer gates. We further compare two constraint encoding strategies for the TSP, encoding only a subset of constraints versus all constraints into the initial state preparation, and show that the former combined with the proposed mixer yields markedly more compact circuits at the point where comparable solution quality is achieved.

</details>


### [126] [Emergent mirror symmetry in the optimization of the central-spin quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11557)
*Hui-Yu Yang,Kun Zhang,Xiao-Hui Wang,Hai-Long Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于中心自旋模型的量子电池优化问题，发现充电性能由两个互补结构指标（平均充电功率上界与储能累积）共同优化，最优点在特定初态激发数对应的Dicke扇区，此时有效充电哈密顿量呈镜像对称性，且该最优动力学可由自旋相干态近似实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池为探索非平衡多体效应在能量存储中的应用提供了有用场景，本文旨在利用中心自旋模型优化量子电池的充电动力学。

Method: 通过识别两个互补的结构指标（平均充电功率上界和储能累积）来分析充电动力学，研究不同初态激发数下的Dicke扇区选择，并利用镜像对称性作为结构指标进行优化。

Result: 两个结构指标在某个特定的初态激发数处同时达到最优，该点对应特定的Dicke扇区；最优动力学可由自旋相干态等乘积初态近似实现。

Conclusion: 充电性能、最优态结构与中心自旋量子电池中涌现的对称性之间存在直接联系，对称性可作为交互多体量子系统中高效充电的有用组织原则。

Abstract: Quantum batteries provide a useful setting for exploring nonequilibrium many-body effects in energy storage. Here we investigate the optimization of a quantum battery based on the central-spin model. We identify two complementary structural indicators associated with the effective charging dynamics: one yields an upper bound on the average charging power, while the other characterizes the buildup of stored energy. We show that these two indicators are jointly optimized at a distinguished initial charger excitation number, which selects a particular Dicke sector of the model. At this common optimal point, the effective charging Hamiltonian becomes exactly mirror symmetric, suggesting mirror symmetry as a useful structural indicator for optimizing quantum batteries. We further show that the corresponding optimal dynamics can be closely approximated by product initial states, in particular by spin coherent states whose excitation-number distribution is centered at the symmetry-selected point. Our results establish a direct connection between charging performance, optimal-state structure, and emergent symmetry in the central-spin quantum battery, and suggest symmetry as a useful organizing principle for efficient charging in interacting many-body quantum systems.

</details>


### [127] [Diffusive Relaxation of Participation Entropy in U(1)-symmetric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11561)
*Hanchen Liu,Tianci Zhou,Xiao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了U(1)守恒律如何通过缓慢的水动力学模式减缓参与熵（PE）的弛豫，建立了PE与密度关联函数的关系，并预言了扩散控制的t^{-1/2}标度律，通过精确计算和张量网络模拟验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究守恒律（尤其是U(1)对称性）如何影响多体波函数在构型空间中的展宽弛豫动力学，揭示参与熵弛豫与流体动力学模式的深层关联。

Method: 基于平衡态附近的集团展开（cluster expansion），将PE亏损与平方连通密度关联函数建立联系；利用精确计算和无穷系统张量网络模拟对量子U(1)守恒电路进行验证。

Result: 在局域密度不均匀性衰减后，PE亏损由平方连通密度关联主导；水动力学区域弛豫为ΔS(t)~t^{-1/2}，当t≥L^2时交叉到exp[-O(t/L^2)]的指数衰减形式。

Conclusion: 参与熵是探测流体动力学记忆的灵敏探针，慢弛豫行为是守恒律的普适结果。

Abstract: Participation entropy (PE) quantifies the spread of a many-body wavefunction across configuration space. While PE relaxes rapidly in generic chaotic systems, we show that $\mathrm{U}(1)$ conservation laws slow it down by imprinting with the slow hydrodynamic modes. Using a cluster expansion around equilibrium, we show that, after local density inhomogeneities decay, the leading PE deficit is dominated by squared connected density correlations. The long time relaxation is therefore controlled by diffusive correlation spreading, giving $ΔS(t)\sim t^{-1/2}$ in the hydrodynamic regime and crossing over to $\sim \exp[-O(t/L^2)]$ when $t\geq L^2$. We confirm this entropy correlation relation using exact computation and infinite system tensor network simulations in various quantum $\mathrm{U}(1)$ conserving circuits. Our results establish PE as a sensitive probe of hydrodynamic memory and suggest that slow relaxation is a generic consequence of conservation laws.

</details>


### [128] [Tensor-Network-Based Distributed Quantum Dynamics on Independent Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11579)
*Anurag Dwivedi,Melissa C. Revelle,Daniel S. Lobser,Brian K. McFarland,Edward C. Tortorici,Christopher G. Yale,Susan M. Clark,Philip Richerme,Srinivasan S. Iyengar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量网络的分布式量子计算模拟方法，用于连续变量表示中的化学波包动力学。通过张量网络分解将多维时间演化算符转化为独立低维传播的并行计算任务，并在Sandia国家实验室的离子阱量子计算机上实验实现了异步分布式量子过程，利用部分纠缠XX(θ)门将双量子比特门保真度提高30%以上。该方法成功计算了质子化水团簇的振动光谱，与经典结果偏差在4cm⁻¹以内，展示了量子计算在光谱学精度方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算资源有限，难以直接模拟复杂的多维量子动力学问题，尤其是连续变量表示的化学波包动力学。现有方法在处理高维纠缠量子演化时计算开销巨大，且缺乏兼容异构量子-经典计算架构的通用框架。此外，实验振动光谱学对某些小分子团簇（如质子化水团簇）的测量存在困难，理论上也面临挑战，亟需一种量子计算方法提供光谱学精度的计算结果。

Method: 利用张量网络表示多维时间演化算符，将其分解为独立低维子空间的传播，从而将纠缠量子演化转化为可异步执行的并行计算任务。该方法建立了张量网络分解、均匀受控量子电路与异步分布式量子计算之间的直接联系。实验上，在Sandia国家实验室的离子阱量子计算机上实现，电路编译采用原生部分纠缠XX(θ)门替代传统完全纠缠门。

Result: 采用部分纠缠XX(θ)门相比传统完全纠缠门，双量子比特门保真度提高了30%以上。在质子化水团簇的振动光谱量子计算中，所得结果与经典计算结果偏差在4cm⁻¹以内，首次实现了与经典结果一致的光谱学精度。

Conclusion: 张量网络分解方法为分布式量子计算提供了一种高效框架，能够兼容异构量子-经典计算架构，并通过部分纠缠门显著提升电路保真度。该方法在化学波包动力学模拟上展现出光谱学精度的潜力，为解决实验上难以测量的分子振动光谱问题提供了新的量子计算途径。

Abstract: We present an approach based on tensor networks for distributed quantum computing simulation of chemical wavepacket dynamics in a continuous variable representation. The central idea is that the tensor-network representation of the multidimensional time-evolution operator naturally induces an elevated Hilbert space where the dynamics decomposes into a set of independent lower-dimensional propagations. This transformation converts an entangled quantum evolution into a set of parallel computational tasks that can be executed asynchronously across heterogeneous quantum and classical computing architectures. The resulting formalism establishes a direct connection between tensor-network decompositions, uniformly controlled quantum circuits, and asynchronous distributed quantum computing. The approach is developed with a goal towards hybrid quantum/classical implementation, and is appropriate for a general heterogeneous mixture of quantum hardware systems. The experimental realization of the asynchronously distributed quantum processes that arise from the tensor-network decomposition are carried out on the Sandia National Laboratories' trapped-ion quantum computer, where the circuits are compiled using native partial-entangling $XX(θ)$ gates, reducing the expected two-qubit gate infidelity by more than 30\% relative to conventional fully entangling decompositions. We demonstrate the methodology by quantum computing the vibrational spectra of a small protonated water cluster that shows critical quantum nuclear behavior. Such water cluster systems have been found to be challenging for experimental action spectroscopy and for theory, and here, for the first time, we provide results for vibrational spectroscopy that are in agreement with the respective classical results to within 4cm$^{-1}$, thus allowing for the potential for spectroscopic accuracy from quantum computations.

</details>


### [129] [Superspace Concentration and Adversarial Robustness in Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11580)
*Eric Yocam,Christian Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan,Yong Wang,Mahesh Kalappattil,Anthony Rizi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统研究了超空间浓度(superspace concentration)作为一种量子资源，基于焦点度量F(ρ)=λ_max(ρ_super)——即约化超空间态的最大本征值——来量化量子系统将信息权重集中到扩展自由度空间中某一优先子空间的能力。通过GPU加速数值模拟验证了该度量的性质：解析退相干预测在超空间维度dS∈{2,4,8,16,32}上达到机器精度(1.11×10^{-16})；焦点单调性在10,000个随机态下零违反；聚焦量子态对相干酉攻击的鲁棒性显著优于标准保真度(焦点在ε=0.302时仍>0.9，而保真度在ε=0.174已下降)；焦点度量与U(dS)-不对称性度量在操作上截然不同；通过恒等式F(|ψ_k⟩⟨ψ_k|)=P(marked)建立了Grover算法与超空间浓度的联系，为oracle查询复杂度提供了资源论解释；最后首次给出了焦点容量缺口ΔF的数值表征，发现其满足log₂(dS)标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子资源理论框架主要关注纠缠、相干性、不对称性等资源，但尚缺乏对量子系统将信息权重集中到扩展自由度空间中某一优先子空间这一能力的系统性资源论研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立超空间浓度作为量子资源的完整理论框架。

Method: 定义焦点度量F(ρ)=λ_max(ρ_super)为约化超空间态的最大本征值；建立资源论公理体系（单调性、可转换性等）；采用GPU加速数值模拟验证理论预言，包括随机态采样、退相干通道作用、相干酉攻击测试等。

Result: (1) 解析退相干预测在dS∈{2,4,8,16,32}上达到机器精度1.11×10^{-16}；(2) 焦点单调性在10,000个随机态下零违反；(3) 焦点对相干攻击的鲁棒性远优于保真度(ε=0.302 vs 0.174)；(4) 焦点度量与U(dS)-不对称性度量操作上截然不同；(5) 建立了F(|ψ_k⟩⟨ψ_k|)=P(marked)的Grover关联；(6) 焦点容量缺口ΔF满足log₂(dS)标度律。

Conclusion: 超空间浓度是一种合法的、具有物理意义的量子资源，焦点度量F(ρ)是表征该资源的有效工具，具有严格的单调性和可操作性。该框架为理解量子算法（如Grover搜索）的加速机制提供了新的资源论视角，且与现有不对称性资源度量在操作上互补。

Abstract: We study superspace concentration as a quantum resource, formalized through the focus measure F(\r{ho}) = λ_max(\r{ho}_super) - the largest eigenvalue of the reduced superspace state - which quantifies the capacity of a quantum system to concentrate informational weight into a preferred subspace of an extended degree-of-freedom space. We develop a complete resource-theoretic framework around this measure and validate its properties through GPU-accelerated numerical simulation. Analytic decoherence predictions are confirmed to machine precision (1.11 x 10^{-16}) for superspace dimensions dS in {2,4,8,16,32}. Focus monotonicity holds across 10,000 random states with zero violations under four focus-non-generating channels across six system configurations. Focused quantum states resist coherent unitary attacks with significantly greater resilience than standard fidelity predicts, with focus remaining above 0.9 at attack strength ε = 0.302 versus ε = 0.174 for fidelity. We further demonstrate that the focus measure and the U(dS)-asymmetry measure are operationally distinct: asymmetry remains near zero and provides no robustness signal under coherent and targeted attacks while focus tracks spectral concentration and remains robust until ε > 0.3. The connection between Grover's algorithm and superspace concentration is made explicit via the identity F(|ψ_k><ψ_k|) = P(marked), providing a resource-theoretic interpretation of oracle query complexity. Finally, we provide the first numerical characterization of the focus capacity gap ΔF, identifying a log_2(dS) scaling law confirmed for both product and correlated noise channels.

</details>


### [130] [Family-Aware Residual Architecture for Predicting Quantum Circuit Simulation Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11620)
*Honjar Xing,Yehong Jiang,Xianbang Wang,Zehua Wang,Zhicheng Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于算法族感知的神经网络架构，用于预测量子电路模拟所需的最小近似阈值和运行时间，在7-130量子比特、10个算法族上实现79.5%精确阈值预测和R²=0.82运行时相关性，推理仅需约50ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似张量网络模拟器在量子电路模拟中虽能超越精确方法，但选择最优近似参数（如键维阈值）依赖昂贵的试错过程，缺乏高效预测手段。

Method: 设计族感知神经网络架构，包含预训练族分类器（97.5%准确率）和基于门组合启发式的算法指纹特征，采用共享骨干网络上的族条件残差校正（基于条件计算技术），同时捕捉通用电路属性和算法族特有特征。

Result: 在7-130量子比特、10个算法族上实现79.5%精确阈值准确率（91.2%在一个梯级内）和R²=0.82运行时相关性，推理仅需约50ms；消融实验表明族感知建模带来最大性能提升（+3.2个百分点）。

Conclusion: 算法族是量子电路模拟成本预测的一等特征，族感知建模可有效替代传统试错式模拟过程，大幅降低时间成本。

Abstract: Approximate tensor-network simulators enable classical simulation of quantum circuits beyond the reach of exact methods, but selecting optimal approximation parameters -- such as bond dimension thresholds -- remains a costly trial-and-error process. We present a family-aware neural architecture that predicts both the minimum approximation threshold required to achieve target fidelity and the expected wall-clock runtime for quantum circuit simulation, given only the circuit's OpenQASM description and execution context. Our key insight is that quantum circuits from different algorithmic families (e.g., QFT, Grover, VQE) exhibit fundamentally distinct simulation cost profiles due to their differing entanglement structures. We employ family-conditioned residual corrections -- additive, family-specific adjustments atop a shared backbone, drawing on established conditional computation techniques -- enabling the model to capture both universal circuit properties and algorithmic nuances. The architecture incorporates a pretrained family classifier (97.5% accuracy) and domain-informed algorithm fingerprint features derived from gate-composition heuristics. Evaluated on circuits spanning 7--130 qubits across 10 algorithm families, our system achieves 79.5% exact threshold accuracy (91.2% within one rung) and $R^2 = 0.82$ runtime correlation, with inference completing in approximately 50 ms -- replacing trial-and-error simulation runs that may take minutes to hours. Ablation studies confirm that family-aware modeling provides the single largest performance improvement (+3.2 percentage points), validating the hypothesis that algorithm family is a first-class feature for simulation cost prediction.

</details>


### [131] [Raw-Curve Quantum Fingerprints: A Mahalanobis Authentication Framework with Drift Early Warning and Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11644)
*Geyuyan Ma,Xiangdong Meng,Yangyang Fei,Zhiqiang Fan,Hanshi Zhao,Chenhui Wang,Haoran Yang,Weilong Wang,Zheng Shan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多维量子指纹的认证框架，通过直接拼接原始测量数据的统计特征，利用马氏距离最近邻分类器在超导量子处理器上实现100%的良性的设备认证，并能抵抗硬件替换攻击、噪声干扰和白盒对抗攻击，同时支持设备健康监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子云平台用户无法验证其计算任务具体由哪台物理设备执行，缺乏透明性使得恶意攻击者可以通过硬件替换攻击将任务重定向到劣质或替代处理器，威胁量子云计算的信任基础。

Method: 从原始测量数据中构建多维量子指纹：直接拼接互补实验的原始统计数据形成高维特征向量（无需曲线拟合），使用马氏距离最近邻分类器进行设备认证，并输出认证置信度C_claimed。该方法统一了指纹提取、漂移鲁棒认证、主动健康监控和对抗防御。

Result: 在三台超导处理器上历时三周的实验实现了100%的良性认证准确率；在加性各向同性高斯噪声下C_claimed以可预测的速率衰减；在白盒对抗攻击下，同一置信度阈值能以近完美成功率检测L2靶向攻击，并揭示L∞攻击的设备相关经验阈值。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多维量子指纹实现了实用化的量子云平台设备认证，兼具抗漂移、健康监控和对抗防御能力，为可信量子云计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum cloud platforms are poised to deliver powerful computing capabilities, but users have no direct means to verify which physical device executes their workload. This lack of transparency enables hardware substitution attacks, where a malicious adversary could redirect a job to a substituted or inferior processor. We present a general authentication framework that addresses this problem by constructing multi-dimensional quantum fingerprints from raw measurement data. Without any curve fitting, we directly concatenate the raw statistics of complementary experiments into a high-dimensional feature vector that preserves subtle device-specific information. A Mahalanobis nearest-neighbor classifier achieves 100\% benign authentication accuracy on three superconducting processors over a three-week chronological split. The classifier naturally yields an authentication confidence $C_{\mathrm{claimed}}$ which reveals device-specific safety margins and motivates per-device alert thresholds. We assess the framework's robustness under two distinct scenarios. Under additive isotropic Gaussian noise, $C_{\mathrm{claimed}}$ decays predictably at a rate explained by inverse covariance traces, enabling an early warning mechanism. Against white-box adversarial perturbations, the same confidence threshold detects $L_2$ targeted attacks with near-perfect success and reveals device-dependent empirical thresholds for $L_\infty$ attacks, while untargeted and sparse attacks are ineffective. The proposed framework thus unifies fingerprint extraction, drift-resilient authentication, proactive health monitoring, and adversarial defense, offering a practical step toward trustworthy quantum cloud computing.

</details>


### [132] [Fast Adiabatic Quantum Gates via Hyperfine Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11655)
*Jiayin Fan,Xingdong Zhao,Manqi Zhang,Fangfang Xie,Jing Qian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电磁感应透明的绝热CNOT门协议，利用原子超精细中间态来加速绝热演化，在铯原子系统中实现了0.9991以上的保真度，为里德伯原子平台上的快速鲁棒绝热量子门提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热量子计算具有对技术缺陷鲁棒的优点，但面临在量子比特相干时间内保持绝热性同时加速绝热操作的根本挑战。

Method: 提出基于电磁感应透明的绝热CNOT门协议，利用原子超精细中间态（HISs）——通过脉冲优化，在STAY路径中增强绝热性，在TRANSFER路径中加速布居转移。扩展模型至任意数量的态，并评估多个HIS的衰减影响。

Result: 在真实铯原子系统中，实现绝热门保真度超过0.9991，操作时间仅0.3903微秒。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了一种在里德伯原子平台上实现快速且鲁棒的绝热量子门的实用路线，具有通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The appeal of adiabatic quantum computing lies in its intrinsic robustness against various technical imperfections, making it attractive for many quantum information applications. However, it faces a fundamental challenge: accelerating the adiabatic operations while preserving adiabaticity within the qubit coherence time. In this article, we propose an electromagnetically induced transparency-based adiabatic CNOT gate protocol which harnesses atomic hyperfine intermediate states (HISs) to speed up the adiabatic evolution. The HISs, naturally-existed in two-photon transitions, often need to be suppressed due to their significant decay errors. In contrast, this paper introduces a novel method that utilizes appropriately chosen HISs not only to enhance the adiabaticity in STAY pathway but also to accelerate the population transfer in TRANSFER pathway. Through pulse optimization, we achieve adiabatic gate fidelities exceeding 0.9991 within 0.3903 μs in realistic Cs atomic setups. To demonstrate the generality of protocol we further assess the impact of decays from multiple HIS and extend our model to arbitrary number of states, providing a practical route toward fast and robust adiabatic quantum gates in Rydberg-atom platforms.

</details>


### [133] [Higher-Order Token Interactions via Quantum Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11673)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子高阶注意力机制(QHA)，通过数据重上传和非Clifford纠缠器在浅层电路中合成阶-k token交互，证明了其与经典自注意力在表达能力上的分离性，并在遗传上位、学习带噪奇偶和三角检测等任务中以更少参数达到更高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积自注意力单层仅能计算两两(阶-2) token交互，表示阶-k交互需要超二次资源或跨层组合，这限制了模型对高阶依赖关系的捕捉能力。

Method: QHA利用数据重上传和全连接非Clifford纠缠器，在量子电路中合成阶-k token交互，并通过局部单量子比特读出暴露高阶信息；同时设计了局部化变体以保证可训练性。

Result: (i) 证明了表达能力分离：经典自注意力层在mHp=o(N/log log N)时无法表示QHA单头表示的阶-k相关族；(ii) 局部化QHA的梯度方差为Ω(1/poly(n))，无贫瘠高原；(iii) 在6.5倍更少参数下，QHA可泛化所有k≤6的隐藏子集奇偶性，而经典注意力在阶-2后崩溃；(iv) 在三个领域中以最小参数达到噪声上限。

Conclusion: QHA作为一种浅层、硬件可实现的量子注意力机制，在理论表达能力和实际任务上均优于经典自注意力，尤其在需要高阶交互建模的场景中优势显著，为量子-经典混合注意力模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Standard dot-product self-attention computes, in a single layer, only pairwise (order-2) interactions between tokens; representing a generic order-$k$ interaction is known to require either super-quadratic resources in one layer or composition across depth. We introduce \textbf{Quantum Higher-Order Attention (QHA)}, a shallow, hardware-realizable quantum attention head that, via data re-uploading and an all-to-all non-Clifford entangler, synthesizes order-$k$ token interactions inside the circuit and exposes them through a local single-qubit read-out. We prove (i) an expressivity separation: any single standard self-attention layer with embedding dimension $m$, $H$ heads and $p$-bit precision satisfying $mHp=o(N/\log\log N)$ cannot represent the order-$k$ correlation family that one QHA head represents with circuit depth $O(\log k)$ ($O(k)$ two-qubit gates); and (ii) a trainability guarantee for its local-design instantiation: with a local read-out and $O(\log n)$ depth the gradient variance is $Ω(1/\mathrm{poly}(n))$ (no barren plateau), which we confirm empirically -- while being explicit that the more expressive all-to-all instantiation we benchmark is trained empirically and shows exponentially decaying gradients. Empirically, at a $6.5\times$ smaller parameter budget, QHA generalizes hidden-subset parity of every order $k\le6$ from disjoint inputs, whereas the larger classical attention head collapses past order~2; consistent with theory, the size of the advantage tracks the target's Fourier degree - largest for parity and shrinking when low-order structure is present. As an application, QHA serves as a compact high-order interaction detector across three domains - genetic epistasis, learning-parity-with-noise, and graph triangle detection - reaching the noise ceiling at the smallest parameter budget where field-standard linear methods fail.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum Correlation Hierarchy and Teleportation in Dephased Hydrogen Hyperfine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11731)
*Geerthana Thiyagarajan,R. Muthuganesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了氢原子超精细自旋系统在马尔可夫相位噪声下的量子关联动力学，推导了X态家族的解析表达式，证明了纠缠、迹距离测量诱导非局域性和平均导引相干性的严格排序，并展示了退相热超精细态可作为量子隐形传态的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氢原子超精细自旋系统中量子关联（纠缠、非局域性、相干性）在马尔可夫相位噪声下的动力学行为及其相互排序关系，并评估该系统的量子隐形传态能力。

Method: 将电子和质子自旋视为开放二量子比特系统，受各向同性超精细哈密顿量和局域退相位作用，解析求解时间相关密度矩阵，推导X态家族的闭合表达式，计算并发度、Trace MIN和平均导引相干性，并建立它们与可直接测量的泡利自旋关联函数的映射。

Result: 证明了三种量子关联的严格排序C(t) ≤ 𝒩₁(t) ≤ ASC(t)；纠缠最脆弱，会在有限时间内经历突然死亡；Trace MIN在非零布居差下具有退相位免疫冻结特性；ASC是最鲁棒的关联量。退相热超精细态的隐形传态优势窗口与纠缠存活区间完全一致。

Conclusion: 识别了四个不同的动力学区域，建立了无需完整态层析即可实验重建量子关联层次结构的方案，揭示了不同量子关联在噪声环境下的差异化鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of quantum correlations in the hydrogen hyperfine spin system subject to Markovian phase noise. Treating the electron and proton spin degrees of freedom as an open two-qubit system governed by an isotropic hyperfine Hamiltonian and local dephasing, we obtain the exact time-dependent density matrix and derive analytical expressions for the full X-state family. We compute concurrence($C$), trace-distance measurement-induced nonlocality (Trace MIN--$\mathcal{N}_1$), and average steering coherence (ASC) in closed form and establish their strict ordering $ C(t)\leq \mathcal{N}_1(t)\leq \mathrm{ASC}(t) $ at all times. Entanglement is identified as the most fragile resource, undergoing sudden death at a finite time. Trace MIN exhibits dephasing-immune freezing for states with nonzero population imbalance, while ASC is the most robust quantity, persisting longest in every scenario studied.We additionally demonstrate that the dephased thermal hyperfine state serves as a resource for quantum teleportation, deriving a closed-form expression for the average fidelity and establishing that the teleportation advantage window coincides exactly with the entanglement survival interval, $\mathcal{F}_A > 2/3 \Longleftrightarrow \mathcal{C} > 0$, for the full X-state family with maximally mixed marginals. We identify four distinct dynamical regimes and map all three correlation measures onto directly measurable Pauli spin correlators, enabling experimental reconstruction of the full hierarchy without full state tomography.

</details>


### [135] [Random Grover Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11759)
*Dekuan Dong,Jiaxin Ma,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机化Grover搜索算法，直接使用多个约束条件的Oracle而非构建全局Oracle，证明了在均匀采样下经过Θ(π/4√(N/r))次迭代后成功概率趋近于1，达到了与标准Grover算法相同的渐近查询复杂度，并推广到任意采样分布和任意数量的Grover算子，还证明了高度偏斜的采样分布仍能实现近单位成功概率，以及渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法需要为整个目标集合构建全局Oracle，但当目标集合是多个约束条件的交集时，构建全局Oracle代价高昂，而单个约束条件的Oracle往往更容易实现。因此，本文旨在设计一种直接利用这些约束Oracle的随机化Grover搜索算法，避免构建全局Oracle的开销。

Method: 研究了一种随机化Grover搜索算法，每次迭代从多个Grover算子中随机选择一个。对于两个算子且均匀采样的情况，证明了成功概率在Θ(π/4√(N/r))次迭代后趋近于1。通过一个近似期望Grover演化的辅助算子，将分析推广到任意采样分布和任意数量的Grover算子。进一步分析了高度偏斜的采样分布的情况，并证明了渐近最优性。

Result: 所提出的随机化Grover搜索算法在无需全局Oracle的前提下，达到了与标准Grover搜索相同的渐近查询复杂度Θ(√(N/r))。高度偏斜的采样分布仍能实现近单位成功概率，使得更廉价的Grover算子可以被更频繁地使用。理论结果得到了数值模拟的支持。

Conclusion: 该随机化Grover搜索算法在避免构建全局Oracle的同时保持了与标准Grover算法相同的渐近最优查询复杂度，特别适用于目标集为多个约束条件交集且单个约束Oracle易于实现的场景，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm achieves a quadratic speedup for unstructured search given a global oracle for the target set. In many applications, however, the target set is specified as the intersection of multiple constraint sets. Constructing a global oracle for the intersection can be costly, whereas the individual constraint oracles are often much simpler to implement.
  We study a randomized Grover search algorithm that directly uses these constraint oracles. At each iteration, one of the corresponding Grover operators is selected at random. For the two-operator case with uniform sampling, we prove that the success probability approaches one after \[ Θ\left(\frac\pi4\sqrt{\frac{N}{r}}\right) \] iterations, where $r$ is the size of the intersection. Thus, the algorithm achieves the same asymptotic query complexity as standard Grover search but without requiring a global oracle. We then generalize the analysis to arbitrary sampling distributions and an arbitrary number of Grover operators through an auxiliary operator that approximates the expected Grover evolution, while retaining the same asymptotic complexity. We further show that highly biased sampling distributions can still achieve near-unit success probability, enabling cheaper Grover operators to be used more frequently. Finally, we prove asymptotic optimality and support the theoretical results with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [136] [Enhancing Many-Body Chaos via Entropy Injection from Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11784)
*Yuke Zhang,Wenbo Zhou,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现环境熵注入（通过热量或粒子转移）可以增强多体混沌，与之前认为的环境抑制混沌的观点不同。作者通过可解的布朗SYK模型，解析计算了稳态量子Lyapunov指数，证明熵流扩大了系统有效希尔伯特空间，从而增强量子置乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭量子系统中，局部信息在幺正演化下扩散并变得复杂；而当系统耦合到环境时，环境-系统耦合可将信息从系统转移到环境，降低系统复杂度增长率（环境诱导置乱转变）。本文旨在识别一种相反的物理过程——来自环境的熵注入，它反而能够增强多体混沌。

Method: 构建可解的复杂布朗SYK模型：将一个已与环境1平衡的系统耦合到另一个环境2（熵库），驱动系统进入非平衡态。通过热量或粒子转移使熵流入系统，扩大系统探索的有效希尔伯特空间。解析计算弛豫过程和稳态量子Lyapunov指数。

Result: 熵注入（通过热转移或粒子转移）扩大了系统有效希尔伯特空间，从而增强多体混沌；在布朗SYK模型中，稳态量子Lyapunov指数可以解析计算，量化了混沌增强效应。

Conclusion: 提供了一种通过熵流调控量子置乱的可控机制，揭示了在耦合环境的多体量子系统中熵注入对混沌的增强作用。

Abstract: In closed quantum systems, local information spreads throughout the entire system and becomes highly complex under unitary evolution. In contrast, when the system is embedded in an environment, system-environment coupling can transfer information from the system into the environment, thereby reducing the rate of complexity growth within the system. This leads to the environment-induced scrambling transition established in previous works. In this work, we identify entropy injection from the environment as a different physical process that instead enhances many-body chaos. Our setup consists of coupling a system that is already in equilibrium with one environment to another environment, which serves as an entropy reservoir and drives the system into a non-equilibrium state. When entropy flows into the system through either heat transfer or particle transfer, the effective Hilbert space explored by the system enlarges, a mechanism that can enhance many-body chaos. We explicitly demonstrate this idea by constructing a solvable complex Brownian SYK model, in which both the relaxation toward the steady state and the steady-state quantum Lyapunov exponent can be computed analytically. Our results provide a controllable mechanism for tuning quantum scrambling through entropy flow in quantum many-body systems coupled to environments.

</details>


### [137] [Sparsified Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Interpretable Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11814)
*Xinge Wu,Huaxin Wang,Jiajun Liu,Ruiqing He,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将稀疏化KAN用于量子态层析，通过分析其内部结构能否恢复已知的Pauli测量关系，证明KAN可在神经重建模型中提供通路级结构可解释性，而非仅作为回归器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习量子态层析方法虽能实现高保真度重建，但训练模型所采用的物理结构往往是隐式的、不可检查的。作者希望探究能否利用KAN不仅实现回归，还能作为可检查的重建规则，让内部组织与已知Pauli结构进行比对。

Method: 在受控的三量子比特GHZ态基准上，使用全部63个非单位Pauli期望值重建三个GHZ子空间变量（z, c, s）。结合有限采样和去极化噪声，通过外部消融从63个测量中识别出扩展的12通道GHZ相关Pauli集，并在多种随机种子和噪声水平下分析支持模式的稳定性，以及进行随机标签对照和稀疏公式恢复。

Result: 外部消融能在各测试条件下精确恢复top-12的GHZ相关Pauli集；支持模式在多随机初始化和噪声水平下保持稳定，并在随机标签对照下崩塌；剪枝后的输入-隐藏-输出通路将Z型布居数可观测量和X/Y非对角可观测量组织成与解析GHZ Pauli分组一致的模式；稀疏公式恢复恢复了带符号的规范Pauli关系。

Conclusion: KAN的贡献在于在神经重建模型中提供通路级结构可解释性，而非实现更优的稀疏回归。结合负对照实验，这些探针提供了一套一致性链条，用于审计学习到的重建规则是否与已知物理结构相符。

Abstract: Machine-learning approaches to quantum state tomography can achieve high reconstruction fidelity, but the physical structure used by the trained model often remains implicit. Here we ask whether a sparsified Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) can be used not only as a regressor, but also as an inspectable reconstruction rule whose internal organization can be checked against known Pauli structure. We study a controlled three-qubit GHZ-family benchmark in which all 63 non-identity Pauli expectation values are used to reconstruct three GHZ-subspace variables: the population imbalance $z$, the real off-diagonal component $c$, and the imaginary off-diagonal component $s$.
