Under finite-shot sampling and depolarizing noise, external ablation identifies the extended 12-channel GHZ-relevant Pauli set from the 63 measurements, with exact top-12 recovery across the tested shot counts and depolarizing-noise strengths. These support patterns remain stable across multi-seed random-initialization and noise-level analyses, and collapse under random-label controls. The dominant pruned input-hidden-output pathways organize Z-type population observables and X/Y off-diagonal observables in a pattern consistent with the analytic GHZ Pauli grouping, and sparse formula recovery recovers the canonical signed Pauli relations. The contribution of the KAN is therefore pathway-level structural interpretability within a neural reconstruction model, rather than superior sparse regression. Together with negative controls, these probes provide a consistency chain for auditing learned reconstruction rules against known physical structure.

</details>


### [138] [Large Fluctuations in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11822)
*V. Yu. Mylnikov,S. O. Potashin,A. Kamenev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了驱动耗散开放量子系统稳态中异常测量结果的统计特性，发现其大偏差函数在相空间中出现非解析性（导数不连续），并以参量驱动Kerr振子为例，揭示了多种瞬子轨迹竞争导致主导瞬子切换，从而产生非解析特征的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索驱动耗散开放量子系统稳态中，异常测量结果统计特性与平衡态系统的本质区别，特别是大偏差函数的解析性质变化。

Method: 基于Keldysh-Lindblad作用量的半经典瞬子轨迹方法，分析参量驱动Kerr振子在线性/非线性耗散浴耦合下的振幅与相位稀有涨落。

Result: 发现驱动耗散系统中大偏差函数普遍存在导数不连续的线或面（非解析性）；同一稀有涨落可由多条不同瞬子轨迹实现；轨迹竞争引发主导瞬子突变，导致非解析特征。

Conclusion: 驱动耗散开放量子系统的稳态统计特性与平衡态有本质区别，大偏差函数的非解析性是一种普适特征，源于多瞬子轨迹竞争机制。

Abstract: We study statistics of atypical measurement outcomes in the steady states of driven open quantum systems. In equilibrium, the probability distribution over the phase space, as encoded in, e.g., the Wigner function, is analytic in the phase-space coordinates. We show that this property is generically lost in driven dissipative systems: their {\it large-deviation function} develops lines and surfaces across which its derivatives are discontinuous. As an illustrative example, we consider a parametrically driven Kerr oscillator coupled linearly and/or nonlinearly to a dissipative bath. Rare fluctuations in the amplitude and phase of the induced oscillations are governed by semiclassical instanton trajectories of the corresponding Keldysh-Lindblad action. We demonstrate that a given fluctuation can be realized through multiple distinct instanton trajectories. The competition between these trajectories leads to abrupt switching of the dominant instanton and, consequently, to non-analytic features in the large-deviation function.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum iterative approach to the Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11843)
*Arturo Rodríguez-Almazán,Guillermo Rivas,Ricardo S. Alonso,Daniela Falcó,Mir Amir Hosseini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种结合量子相位估计（QPE）和Grover搜索算法的量子迭代框架来解决旅行商问题（TSP），通过将路径成本编码为量子相位，利用QPE评估成本，并通过Grover-Long算法进行振幅放大以迭代优化解空间。在小规模TSP实例上的概念验证展示了可行性，预期计算复杂度为O(m²log₂(m)log₂(1/ε)/√ε)，依赖于误差容限ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题（TSP）是经典的NP难组合优化问题，随着问题规模增大，计算变得不可行。现有方法主要依赖量子退火，但不够高效，因此探索量子计算的新途径来解决TSP的复杂性。

Method: 提出量子迭代框架，将TSP路径成本编码为量子相位，使用量子相位估计（QPE）高效评估成本，再通过Grover-Long算法实现振幅放大（Amplitude Amplification），迭代地缩小解空间以逼近最优路径。

Result: 在小规模TSP实例上进行了概念验证，展示了该方法的可行性。预期计算复杂度为O(m²log₂(m)log₂(1/ε)/√ε)，其中ε为误差容限参数，该估计未包含初始化项。

Conclusion: 该量子迭代框架在解决TSP问题上具有可行性，并有望扩展到更大规模的优化问题。未来改进有望使初始化项相对于相位估计项变得可忽略。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a classical NP-hard problem in combinatorial optimization, where determining the shortest route among a set of cities becomes computationally prohibitive as the problem size increases. This work explores quantum computing as an alternative approach to address this complexity. Unlike existing methods that primarily rely on quantum annealing, we propose a quantum iterative framework integrating Quantum Phase Estimation (QPE) and Grover's search algorithm. Route costs are encoded as quantum phases, enabling QPE to efficiently evaluate them, while Amplitude Amplification, implemented via the Grover-Long algorithm, iteratively refines the solution space toward the optimal route. A proof-of-concept case study on a small-scale TSP instance demonstrates the feasibility of this approach and its potential for scaling to larger optimization problems. Furthermore, under an expectation-based analysis, the algorithm exhibits an expected computational complexity of $O(\frac{m^2\log_2(m)\log_2(1/ε)}{\sqrtε})$ which depends on the error tolerance parameter $ε$. This estimation omits the initialization term, which we expect future refinements to render subdominant to Phase Estimation.

</details>


### [140] [Tensor-Network Algorithm for Many-Body Trace Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11882)
*Seunghun Lee,Eun-Gook Moon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量网络的受控算法，用于估计矩阵乘积算子的迹范数，无需完全对角化，通过结合Zolotarev有理近似和变分公式（类似DMRG算法）实现，在纠缠负性、量子保真度和量子Fisher信息等计算中展现出远超精确对角化能力的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迹范数是量子信息理论中的基本量，但在多体系统中计算迹范数通常需要对角化指数级大小的算子，成为主要的计算瓶颈，亟需一种可扩展的高效算法。

Method: 将Zolotarev对符号函数的有理近似与变分公式相结合，采用类似密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）的算法来求解，实现对矩阵乘积算子迹范数的受控估计，近似精度可通过有理近似参数和近零谱权重进行系统调控。

Result: 在超越精确对角化能力范围的体系中，成功实现了纠缠负性、量子保真度和量子Fisher信息的受控迹范数计算，精度显著优于基于多项式的Lanczos方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于迹范数的物理量确立为实用的张量网络可观测量，为混合态量子信息的张量网络研究开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Trace norms are fundamental to quantum information theory, yet in many-body systems their evaluation remains a major computational bottleneck, as it generally requires diagonalizing exponentially large operators. Here, we overcome this bottleneck by introducing a controlled tensor-network algorithm for estimating the trace norm of matrix product operators without full diagonalization. The key idea is to combine Zolotarev's rational approximation to the sign function with a variational formulation solved using a density-matrix-renormalization-group-like algorithm. The resulting approximation is systematically improvable, with its accuracy controlled by the rational approximation parameters and the spectral weight near zero. Beyond the reach of exact diagonalization, we demonstrate controlled trace-norm calculations for entanglement negativity, quantum fidelity and quantum Fisher information, achieving substantially improved accuracy over polynomial-based Lanczos approaches. Our results establish trace-norm-based quantities as practical tensor-network observables, opening a route toward tensor-network studies of quantum information in mixed states.

</details>


### [141] [Controlled ion-ion interactions and cavity-enhanced emission of a coherent dinuclear Eu$^{3+}$ complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11947)
*Evgenij Vasilenko,Vishnu Unni Chorakkunnath,Barbora Brachnakova,Nicholas Lester Jobbitt,Senthil Kumar Kuppusamy,David Hunger,Mario Ruben*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种Eu³⁺基分子配合物（单核与双核）的光学相干性和激发诱导相互作用，在双核体系中实现了约7 Å的离子间距控制，展示了长达9 μs的光学相干时间，并通过控制-靶序列证明了离子间条件性相互作用，同时在光纤微腔中实现了380倍的发射增强，为可扩展多量子比特架构提供了化学可调的分子平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用分子稀土离子配合物的化学可调性，构建具有定义量子比特耦合的多量子比特架构，以实现双量子比特量子门，从而推动可扩展量子技术的发展。

Method: 对单核和双核Eu³⁺分子配合物进行低温（最低100 mK）系综光谱学表征，包括光谱烧孔、自由感应衰减和光子回波测量；实施控制-目标序列探测离子间条件性相互作用；将双核配合物集成到光纤基光学微腔中。

Result: 实现了长达9 μs的光学相干时间T₂,ₒ；双核配合物中观察到更强的相互作用诱导退相干；在光纤微腔中观测到⁵D₀→⁷F₀跃迁的380倍发射增强。

Conclusion: 分子稀土配合物可作为化学可调、可扩展的量子技术构建模块，为多量子比特架构的实现提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Molecular rare-earth-ion complexes offer unique opportunities for quantum technologies by combining the intrinsic coherence properties of rare-earth ions with chemically tunable molecular environments. A crucial capability is the realization of multi-qubit architectures with defined qubit couplings to enable two-qubit quantum gates. Here, we investigate the optical coherence properties and excitation-induced interactions of two Eu$^{3+}$-based molecular complexes, comparing a mononuclear reference system with a dinuclear analogue in which two Eu$^{3+}$ ions are positioned at a well-defined intramolecular distance of about 7 Angstrom. Using cryogenic ensemble spectroscopy, including spectral hole burning, free-induction decay, and photon echo measurements at temperatures down to 100 mK, we demonstrate long optical coherence times $T_{2,\text{o}}$ of up to 9 $μ$s. As a key step toward scalable multi-qubit architectures, a control-target sequence was implemented to probe conditional ion-ion interactions, revealing a stronger interaction-induced dephasing in the dinuclear complex. Finally, we show the integration of the dinuclear complex into a fiber-based optical microcavity, and observe an 380-fold emission enhancement of the $\mathrm{}^5\mathrm{D}_0\rightarrow\mathrm{}^7\mathrm{F}_0$ transition. Together, these results position molecular rare-earth complexes as versatile and chemically tunable building blocks for scalable quantum technologies.

</details>


### [142] [Super-Heisenberg Non-Equilibrium Quantum Sensing with Waveguide-Coupled Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11975)
*Mohammad B. Arjmandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合到一维光子波导的量子发射器阵列作为非平衡探针，通过优化发射器位置和间距，利用集体辐射动力学实现超越海森堡极限的量子 Fisher 信息增强，展示了波导耦合发射器阵列作为多功能量子传感器的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用量子发射器阵列作为非平衡探针来估计波导特性（如波数），并探索如何通过发射器定位和集体辐射动力学来增强量子 Fisher 信息，以实现更高精度和更长持续时间的量子传感。

Method: 通过考虑初始激发后的瞬态动力学，分析了双发射器和多发射器探针的量子 Fisher 信息。研究发现最优间距可稳定单激发子空间中的布居和相干性，抑制超辐射衰减；对随机发射器配置的分析表明，消失的波导介导交叉衰减可最大化敏感性和信息可访问时间窗口。扩展到多体探针时，分析了 QFI 及其时间积分随系统规模的标度关系。

Result: 双发射器探针中，最优间距可显著增强 QFI 的大小和持续时间；随机配置中，最小化波导介导的交叉衰减可最大化敏感性和信息可访问时间；多体探针中，最大 QFI 及其时间积分随系统规模增长，超越了海森堡极限。

Conclusion: 波导耦合发射器阵列可作为多功能量子传感器，通过利用集体辐射动力学实现可调谐、长寿命和高精度的参数估计，为超越标准量子极限的量子传感提供了新途径。

Abstract: We explore an array of quantum emitters as non-equilibrium probes, coupled to a one-dimensional photonic waveguide, aiming to estimate its properties such as wave number which encodes the waveguide frequency and dispersive characteristics. By considering transient dynamics following initial excitation, we show that the quantum Fisher information (QFI) can be significantly enhanced through careful emitter positioning. For two-emitter probes, optimal spacing stabilizes populations and coherences in the single-excitation subspace, suppressing super radiant decay and extending both the magnitude and longevity of QFI. Randomized emitter configurations also reveal that vanishing waveguide-mediated cross decay maximizes both achievable sensitivity and the temporal duration over which information about the parameter remains accessible. Extending to multipartite probes, we demonstrate that the maximum QFI and its temporal integral scale with system size, exceeding the Heisenberg limit for all positioning strategies. Our results highlight the potential of waveguide-coupled emitter arrays as versatile quantum sensors, where collective radiative dynamics can be harnessed to achieve tunable, long-lived, and enhanced precision.

</details>


### [143] [Dark state spectroscopy in nonlinear waveguide quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11997)
*Shay Nadel,Amir Sivan,Aviv Karnieli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用χ(2)非线性波导中产生的弱压缩光来测量发射器阵列中完全暗态的光谱，从而克服了量子系统中保持长相干时间与可测量性之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统面临一个根本性权衡：与环境解耦以维持长相干时间，但测量又需要与环境相互作用。现有方法依赖系统非理想性来实现测量，但这会限制相干时间。

Method: 提出使用χ(2)非线性波导中产生的弱压缩光，通过荧光光谱探测发射器阵列中暗态之间的缀饰跃迁。

Result: 荧光光谱能够探测发射器阵列中缀饰暗态之间的跃迁，实现了对完全暗态的测量。

Conclusion: 该工作为暗态的测量和控制铺平了道路，可应用于鲁棒量子存储器、量子计算和量子通信。

Abstract: Quantum systems face a fundamental trade-off: they must remain decoupled from the environment to maintain long coherence times, yet they require interactions with the environment to be accessible for measurement. As a prime example, emitter arrays coupled to waveguides facilitate collective modes that, owing to interference, can suppress radiation into the waveguide. While complete destructive interference creates perfectly dark states with infinite lifetimes, their inherent decoupling makes them unmeasurable in standard waveguide quantum electrodynamics. Consequently, current approaches must rely on system non-idealities that permit measurement but limit the coherence times. In this work, we lift this limitation by proposing the use of weakly squeezed light generated in \{chi}(2) nonlinear waveguides for the spectroscopy of completely dark states. We show that the fluorescence spectrum probes transitions between the dressed dark states of the emitter array. This work paves the way towards the measurement and control of dark states, with applications for robust quantum memories, computation, and communication.

</details>


### [144] [Experimental Tabletop Petz recovery of a photonic qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12020)
*Hui Li,Jinyan Chen,Yue Pan,Liang Xu,Minjeong Song,Valerio Scarani,Lijian Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了Petz恢复映射，用于可调退相干和耗散的多用量子比特通道，首次展示了“桌面可逆性”——即恢复映射可使用与正向耗散演化相同的设备实现，从而资源高效地缓解量子系统中的信息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放演化中丢失的量子信息无法完全恢复，但可实现部分恢复。Petz恢复映射在理论上能保证近乎最优的恢复效果，然而其实验实现十分有限，且通常限于单一固定噪声模型。本文旨在实现一种适用于可调噪声模型的多用量子比特通道的Petz恢复映射。

Method: 描述并实现了一种适用于多用量子比特通道的Petz恢复映射，该通道具有可调的退相干和耗散特性。实验装置首次实现了“桌面可逆性”，即使用与正向耗散演化相同的设备来实现Petz恢复映射。

Result: 对于一定范围的参考态选择，Petz恢复映射可以使用与正向耗散演化相同的设备实现，并能部分抵消其效应。实验表明该方案无需复杂的辅助资源即可资源高效地实现。

Conclusion: Petz恢复映射可以资源高效地实现，无需复杂的辅助量子资源，为缓解量子系统中的信息丢失提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: The quantum information lost in open evolutions cannot be fully recovered, but partial recovery is possible. The Petz recovery map guarantees almost optimal recovery, notably if the chosen reference state is close to the real one. This map has been widely used in theoretical studies, but has been the object of only a handful of experimental realisations, typically under a single fixed noise model. In this work, we describe and implement the Petz recovery map for a versatile class of qubit channels with tunable decoherence and dissipation. The setup we realize is also the first experimental example of ``tabletop reversibility'': for a good range of choices of the reference state, the Petz recovery map can be implemented with the same devices as the forward dissipative evolution, whose effect it is partially undoing. Our results demonstrate that the Petz recovery map can be resource-efficiently realized without requiring complex ancillary resources, providing a feasible pathway for mitigating information loss in quantum systems.

</details>


### [145] [Measurement-Free Toric-Code Memory in Array Globally Controlled Rydberg Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12030)
*Han Wang,Yusheng Zhao,Xiuhao Deng,Jinguo Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需移动、测量或局部寻址的中性原子Rydberg阵列拓扑量子存储协议，利用三物种原子阵列和全局物种选择性激光脉冲实现toric码的完整稳定子循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠错循环需要的中路荧光测量、区间原子输运和局域单量子比特寻址等辅助操作引入了延迟、原子丢失或光学串扰，其代价比底层门操作高出数个数量级，且随循环累积严重退化逻辑信息。

Method: 采用三物种Rydberg原子阵列，在全局物种选择性激光脉冲下完成完整的稳定子循环，包括综合征提取、相干纠错和辅助比特重置，避免了移动、测量和局部寻址操作。

Result: 4×4旋转toric码的数值模拟显示，当物理错误率低于伪阈值p*≈0.034时，量子比特寿命得到延长。

Conclusion: 该方案为中性原子平台提供了一条具体的、硬件高效的拓扑量子存储实现路径。

Abstract: The central prerequisite of any fault-tolerant quantum architecture is a quantum memory: a block of encoded physical qubits whose logical state is actively preserved against noise across many rounds of error correction. In neutral-atom Rydberg arrays, realizing such a memory is obstructed not by the entangling gates themselves, which are already fast and high-fidelity, but by the auxiliary operations that a conventional error-correction cycle requires: mid-circuit fluorescence measurement, inter-zone atom transport, and locally focused single-qubit addressing. Each of these introduces latency, atom loss, or optical crosstalk that exceeds the cost of the underlying gates by orders of magnitude. These costs accumulate cycle after cycle, progressively degrading the very logical information the code is meant to protect. Here we propose a protocol that stabilizes a toric-code quantum memory without moving, measuring or local addressing atoms. The key is to use a three-species Rydberg atom array for the complete stabilizer cycle, including syndrome extraction, coherent correction, and ancilla reset, under global, species-selective laser pulses. Numerical simulation of a $4 \times 4$ rotated toric code shows a longer qubit lifetime when the physical error rate is below a pseudo-threshold $p^\star \approx 0.034$. The scheme offers a concrete, hardware-efficient route to topological quantum memory in neutral-atom platforms.

</details>


### [146] [Shadow Engineering of Quantum Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12035)
*Tian-Ci Tian,De-Tao Jiang,Wei-Ming Zhu,Wei-You Liao,Hong-Wei Li,He-Liang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了影子工程（shadow engineering）框架，通过将量子过程的经典影子编码为稀疏转移矩阵，实现了对复合量子过程 $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$ 性质的高效预测，在保持单通道效率的同时实现指数级低于量子过程层析的样本复杂度，并在超导量子处理器上验证了其在误差缓解和哈密顿动力学模拟等应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子过程经典影子方法仅适用于单通道过程 $\mathcal{E}$ 的性质预测，尚未探索如何高效预测复合过程 $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$ 的性质，亟需一种无需物理执行复合过程即可预测其性质的框架。

Method: 将每个子过程 $\mathcal{E}_m$ 的经典影子数据编码为稀疏转移矩阵，通过矩阵运算高效预测复合过程 $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$ 的任意线性/非线性性质，并给出多项式样本复杂度的理论保证。

Result: 理论证明该框架的样本复杂度与单通道过程经典影子方法匹配，且指数级低于量子过程层析；在超导量子处理器上实验验证了其在误差缓解和哈密顿动力学模拟中的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 影子工程框架无需物理重执行即可预测复杂量子行为，为近期的器件校准和量子模拟等应用提供了新的能力。

Abstract: Characterizing quantum processes is essential for hardware benchmarking, error diagnosis, and algorithm verification. While recent work [PRX QUANTUM \textbf{4}, 040337 (2023)] extended classical shadows from quantum state to quantum process, enabling efficient single-channel $\mathcal{E}$ property prediction, its applicability to composite processes $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$ remains unexplored. We introduce shadow engineering, a framework encoding the classical shadows of processes into sparse transfer matrices to predict $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$ properties with proven polynomial sample complexity, matching single-channel efficiency while exponentially lower than quantum process tomography. Crucially, this approach repurposes existing $\mathcal{E}_m$-shadow data without physical execution of $f(\mathcal{E}_1, \mathcal{E}_2,\cdots, \mathcal{E}_k)$, enabling flexible quantum process characterization with minimal hardware overhead. We demonstrate the framework's effectiveness and practicality on a superconducting quantum processor for typical applications such as error mitigation and Hamiltonian dynamical simulation. This framework unlocks new capabilities for predicting complex quantum behaviors without physical re-execution, with immediate applications in near-term device calibration and quantum simulation.

</details>


### [147] [A Geometric Family of Correlations Containing the Quantum Singlet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12045)
*E. Aldo Arroyo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何约束隐变量框架，生成由边界函数参数化的关联族，其中量子单态关联是特例。推导了精确关联函数，建立了可容许性条件、对称性、CHSH函数的通用驻点，以及ν=π/4处CHSH值与隐变量分布几何对比度之间的精确关系。该框架将量子单态关联置于更广泛的几何结构中，暗示存在非平凡几何结构，并激励寻找从该族中选择量子单态解的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子单态关联通常被视为孤立目标进行再现，缺乏将其置于更广泛结构中进行理解的框架。本文旨在构建一个几何约束隐变量框架，将量子单态关联嵌入一个更大的关联族中，从而揭示其在该族中的特殊地位，并为寻找选择该解的原理奠定基础。

Method: 提出一个几何约束隐变量框架，通过边界函数参数化生成关联族；推导关联函数的精确解析表达式；建立可容许性条件、对称性性质；分析CHSH函数的通用驻点；推导ν=π/4处CHSH值与隐变量分布几何对比度之间的精确关系。

Result: 得到了关联函数的精确表达式；建立了可容许性条件和对称性性质；发现CHSH函数存在一个与边界函数无关的通用驻点；推导出ν=π/4处CHSH值与隐变量分布几何对比度之间的精确关系式。

Conclusion: 量子单态关联并非孤立现象，而是嵌入在一个更大的几何约束关联族中；存在非平凡的几何结构支配这些关联；有必要寻找一个基本原理，能够从该族中唯一选出量子单态解。

Abstract: We introduce a geometrically constrained hidden-variable framework that generates a family of correlations parametrized by a boundary function, within which the quantum singlet correlation appears as a particular member. Exact expressions for the correlation function are derived. Several structural results are established, including admissibility conditions, symmetry properties, a universal stationary point of the associated CHSH function, and an exact relation between the CHSH value at $ν=π/4$ and a geometric contrast measure defined on the underlying hidden-variable distributions. Rather than treating the quantum singlet correlation as an isolated target to be reproduced, the present framework places it within a broader geometric structure of correlations. These results suggest the existence of a nontrivial geometric structure underlying the family of correlations and motivate the search for a principle capable of selecting the quantum singlet solution from within that family.

</details>


### [148] [Clifford disentanglers for entanglement reduction in molecular electronic structure simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12056)
*Longfei Chang,Zibo Wu,Yunzhi Li,Haiqi Liu,Jiajun Ren,Mingpu Qin,Zhendong Li,Wei-Hai Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统评估并扩展了Clifford解纠缠器作为结构保持的纠缠约简工具，通过分类双/四量子比特Clifford算子（分别降至20和91392个代表元），在迭代Clifford增强矩阵乘积态框架中降低固定键维下的能量误差，并减轻对轨道排序和费米子-量子比特映射的依赖，同时验证其对量子模拟（如浅层VQE）的益处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠是限制张量网络和量子模拟分子电子结构效率的关键瓶颈，现有方法缺乏能同时保持哈密顿量Pauli-弦形式的结构保持纠缠约简手段。

Method: 对Clifford算子按二分Schmidt谱作用分类，将两/四量子比特搜索空间压缩至20和91392个代表元；嵌入迭代Clifford增强矩阵乘积态框架中进行纠缠约简。

Result: Clifford解纠缠器在固定键维下降低分子测试案例的能量误差，减轻对轨道排序和费米子-量子比特映射的依赖，并提升浅层VQE计算效果。

Conclusion: Clifford解纠缠器是一种有效的结构保持纠缠工程工具，可用于张量网络和量子模拟分子电子结构，同时阐明了其相关性依赖并为未来改进指明方向。

Abstract: Entanglement is a key bottleneck limiting the efficiency of tensor-network and quantum simulations of molecular electronic structures. Here, we systematically assess and extend Clifford disentanglers as a structure-preserving approach to entanglement reduction: they can modify the entanglement structure of qubit wavefunctions while retaining the Pauli-string form of qubit Hamiltonians. To enable a practical search over Clifford transformations, we classify Clifford operators by their action on the Schmidt spectrum across a bipartition, reducing the two- and four-qubit search spaces to 20 and 91392 representatives, respectively. Embedded in an iterative Clifford-augmented matrix product state framework, these transformations reduce the energy errors at fixed bond dimension for the molecular test cases studied and mitigate the dependence on orbital orderings and fermion-to-qubit mappings. We further show that Clifford disentanglers can also benefit quantum simulations such as the shallow-circuit variational quantum eigensolver calculations. Together, these results establish Clifford disentanglers as a useful structure-preserving entanglement-engineering tool for tensor-network and quantum simulations of molecular electronic structure, while also clarifying their correlation dependence and motivating future developments.

</details>


### [149] [The quantum harmonic oscillator and the real Hilbert space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12060)
*Sergio Giardino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在复数与四元数广义框架下研究谐振子，分别用复位置函数处理经典振子、复波函数和四元数波函数处理量子振子，所有量子解均在实Hilbert空间形式体系下获得。结果表明复数与四元数描述适合处理非平稳过程（如阻尼振荡、受迫振荡）以及无法用其他方式恰当描述的自相互作用过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索复数与四元数框架下谐振子的描述能力，解决传统形式体系难以恰当处理的非平稳过程（阻尼振荡、受迫振荡、自相互作用过程）的描述问题。

Method: 在广义复数与四元数框架下，针对经典谐振子采用复位置函数；针对量子谐振子采用复波函数和四元数波函数，所有量子求解均在实Hilbert空间形式体系内完成。

Result: 复数与四元数描述被证明是适合处理非平稳过程的框架，能够描述阻尼振荡、受迫振荡以及自相互作用过程，而这些过程在传统描述中无法被恰当处理。

Conclusion: 复数与四元数广义框架为描述非平稳谐振子过程（包括阻尼、受迫及自相互作用）提供了有效且必要的数学工具，拓展了经典与量子谐振子的理论描述能力。

Abstract: The harmonic oscillator is considered within generalized frameworks using complex and quaternionic numbers. The classical oscillator is considered in terms of a complex position function, and quantum oscillators are examined in terms of complex wave functions, and in terms of quaternionic wave functions as well. Both of the quantum solutions are obtained within the real Hilbert space formalism. The results reveal the complex and quaternionic descriptions as suitable frameworks for non-stationary processes, including damped oscillations, forced oscillations, and additionally self-interacting processes that cannot be appropriately described otherwise.

</details>


### [150] [A semi-definite programming formulation of the device-dependent guessing probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12079)
*Raffaele D'Avino,Aurora Mugnai,Miguel Navascués,Antonio Acín,Gabriel Senno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半定规划的形式化方法，用于在完全表征但不可信的制备-测量量子系统中，估计Eve能够成功猜测测量结果的最大概率，从而量化系统可证明的量子随机性。该方法被应用于多种场景，包括此前仅知上界的系统，并发现制备装置与测量装置之间的纠缠会增强Eve的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量产生的内在随机性是量子随机数生成的基础，但现有的方法无法对完全表征但不可信的制备-测量系统中产生的内在随机性进行一般性估计，因此需要一种通用的量化方法。

Method: 提出了一种半定规划（SDP）形式化方法，以计算在表征但不可信的制备-测量设置中，Eve成功猜测测量结果的最大概率，从而间接量化系统可证明的随机性。

Result: 该方法在多种具体设置中得到了验证，能够确定此前仅知上界的场景中的确切可证明随机量；同时发现，即使是在最基础的二元测量作用于单量子比特态的场景中，制备装置与测量装置之间的纠缠也会增强Eve的预测能力。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为完全表征但不可信的制备-测量量子系统提供了通用的随机性量化方法，并揭示了纠缠在其中的负面影响，为量子随机数生成的安全性评估提供了理论工具。

Abstract: In quantum mechanics, a measurement applied to a state in general produces some amount of intrinsic randomness. This is not only a fundamental feature of the theory, but is also at the basis of any quantum process to generate random numbers. The simplest of such processes consists of a single, fully charaterized, measurement acting on a single, fully characterized, state. Unfortunately, no general method to estimate the intrinsic randomness produced in such setups is known. In this work, we address this issue by presenting a semidefinite programming formulation of the maximum probability with which an adversary, Eve, can guess the outcomes of characterized but untrusted prepare-and-measure setups. We then present several applications of this construction. First, we apply our method to a variety of specific setups, allowing us both to benchmark the approach and, more importantly, to determine the exact amount of certifiable randomness in scenarios where only upper bounds were previously available. Then, we show that the presence of entanglement between the device preparing the state and the measurement strictly increases Eve's predictive power, already in the most elementary setup of a binary measurement acting on a qubit state.

</details>


### [151] [Bound State Solutions of the Relativistic Finite-difference Equation for the Ring-shaped Quesne Oscillator Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12082)
*Sh. M. Nagiyev,Narmin Nasibova,V. A. Tarverdiyeva,G. H. Guliyeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了量子三维环状Quesne振子势的相对论有限差分方程，通过相对论构型r-空间方法，发现径向波函数用连续对偶Hahn多项式表示，角向波函数用Jacobi多项式表示，得到了离散能谱，并建立了径向运动的SU(1,1)动力学对称群以实现纯代数求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子力学框架下，采用有限差分版本而非传统微分方程形式，精确求解三维环状Quesne振子势问题，以探索该势场在相对论情形下的可解性。

Method: 基于相对论有限差分方程，引入相对论构型r-空间概念，分离变量后分别求解径向和角向方程；径向波函数表示为连续对偶Hahn多项式，角向波函数为Jacobi多项式；构建SU(1,1)动力学对称群进行纯代数求解。

Result: 获得了离散能谱；径向波函数和能谱在非相对论极限下正确退化为经典结果；通过SU(1,1)动力学对称群实现了能谱的纯代数推导。

Conclusion: 相对论有限差分方法可精确求解环状Quesne振子势，所得解析解具有正确的非相对论极限，且系统内在的SU(1,1)对称性揭示了该问题的可积性结构。

Abstract: We solve exactly the relativistic finite-difference equation for the quantum three-dimensional ring-shaped Quesne oscillator potential. Our investigation is based on a finite-difference version of relativistic quantum mechanics. So-called relativistic configurational r-space is a key concept here. We show that the radial wavefunctions and angular wavefunctions are expressed through the continuous dual Hahn polynomials and Jacobi polynomials, respectively. A discrete energy spectrum has been found. The radial wave functions and energy spectrum have the correct nonrelativistic limit. We also build a dynamical symmetry group SU (1, 1) for the radial part of the equation of motion, which allows us to find the energy spectrum purely algebraically.

</details>


### [152] [Necessary and Sufficient Conditions for Universal Gates with Pauli Strings and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12096)
*Isaac D. Smith,Hans J. Briegel,Hendrik Poulsen Nautrup*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了由Pauli算符乘积（Pauli字符串）组成的哈密顿量集合生成su(2^n)（即实现n量子比特通用量子计算）的充要条件，以及一般哈密顿量与Pauli字符串组合时的通用性充分条件（特定情况下为必要条件）。作为应用，证明了两个推论：(i) 在任意单量子比特控制下一般哈密顿量通用的充要条件；(ii) XYZ海森堡哈密顿量仅需控制相邻两量子比特即可实现通用量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子计算的通用性提供严格的数学条件，特别是针对Pauli字符串哈密顿量集合和一般哈密顿量在Pauli基展开下的通用性判定。

Method: 利用李代数方法，分析哈密顿量集合生成的李代数结构，基于Pauli算符的乘积性质和Pauli基展开，推导生成su(2^n)的充要条件与充分条件。

Result: 得到了Pauli字符串集合生成su(2^n)的充要条件；给出了一般哈密顿量与Pauli字符串组合时通用性的充分条件（特定情形下为充要条件）；证明了两个关于哈密顿量通用性的推论。

Conclusion: 本文为量子计算通用性提供了系统的代数判定条件，并展示了其在具体物理系统（如XYZ海森堡模型）中的应用价值。

Abstract: Any quantum computation consists of a sequence of unitary evolutions described by a finite set of Hamiltonians. For the case where this set consists of only products of Pauli operators, known as Pauli strings, we provide a necessary and sufficient condition for it to generate $\mathfrak{su}(2^n)$, i.e., to be universal for quantum computation on $n$ qubits. When combining Pauli strings with a general Hamiltonian, we show a sufficient (and in certain circumstances even necessary) condition for universality based on the Pauli-basis expansion of the Hamiltonian. As an application of these results, we prove two corollaries: (i) a necessary and sufficient condition for the universality of a general Hamiltonian given arbitrary single-qubit control on all qubits, and (ii) the universality of an XYZ Heisenberg Hamiltonian with local control of just two adjacent qubits.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum ergodicity and semiclassical measures: mathematical results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12098)
*Stéphane Nonnenmacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了混沌测地流下Laplacian高频本征模的宏观分布，重点介绍了量子遍历性定理的详细证明（含边界情形），并讨论了量子唯一遍历性猜想及其在Anosov系统中的进展，包括Kolmogorov-Sinai熵对半经典测度的约束和最近的反局域化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解混沌系统（测地流混沌）中量子或波动系统的本征模（高频Laplacian本征函数）的宏观分布规律，这是量子混沌领域的核心问题。

Method: 基于半经典测度的概念，详细证明了Schnirelman提出的量子遍历性定理，并针对带边界流形进行了适应性调整；对于Anosov强混沌系统，利用Kolmogorov-Sinai熵等工具分析半经典测度的约束条件。

Result: 量子遍历性定理成立（包括带边界情形）；在Anosov系统上，对可能的半经典测度给出了熵约束，并获得了反局域化（delocalization）的进展。

Conclusion: 量子遍历性定理是描述混沌系统高频本征模分布的基本结果；量子唯一遍历性猜想在强混沌系统中取得进展，但仍有待完全解决。

Abstract: In this chapter we review some results describing the high-frequency eigenmodes of the Laplacian on compact manifolds, or Euclidean domains, for which the geodesic flow is chaotic. We focus on the macroscopic distribution of these eigenmodes, which is described by the concept of semiclassical measure. The main result on the question is the Quantum Ergodicity theorem, originally due to Schnirelman. We provide the detailed proof of this theorem, including the adjustments necessary to treat the case of manifolds with boundary. We also discuss the Quantum Unique Ergodicity conjecture, and some progress towards this conjecture for strongly chaotic (Anosov) systems. In particular, we describe the constraints on admissible semiclassical measures, in terms of their Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy, as well as more recent delocalization results.

</details>


### [154] [On-Chip Quantum Randomness Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12173)
*Lang Li,Yutian Wu,Giulio Chiribella,Ravishankar Ramanathan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在集成硅光子芯片上首次演示了半设备无关随机性放大，采用新型SDI熵认证技术，实现了20 Mbps的吞吐率，并提供了比现有方法更严格的冯·诺依曼熵界，即使在制备和测量设备共享量子关联时依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机性放大是密码学的核心任务，全设备无关量子协议安全性最高但难以集成到可扩展设备中，半设备无关协议是一种有前景的替代方案，但此前尚未在集成光子平台上实现。

Method: 采用新型SDI熵认证技术，在集成硅光子芯片上实现半设备无关随机性放大协议，该技术能够提供更严格的冯·诺依曼熵界，且对制备与测量设备间的量子关联具有鲁棒性。

Result: 首次在集成硅光子芯片上演示SDI随机性放大，实现了20 Mbps的实际应用级吞吐率。

Conclusion: 该工作开发的SDI技术可集成到便携式电信设备中，开启了新一代量子密码硬件的发展方向。

Abstract: Randomness amplification, the task of extracting uniform private bits from biased seeds that may be partly known by a malicious third party, is of central importance in cryptography. The highest security in this task is provided by a class of quantum protocols known as device-independent, which however are challenging to integrate into scalable devices. Semi-device-independent (SDI) protocols are a promising alternative that guarantees security under few natural assumptions, such as bounds on the amount of energy used by the devices. Here, we provide the first demonstration of SDI randomness amplification on an integrated silicon photonic chip, achieving a throughput rate of 20 Mbps suitable for practical applications. This rate is achieved through a novel technique for SDI entropy certification, which delivers strictly tighter von Neumann entropy bounds compared to existing methods and remains valid even if the preparation and measurement devices share quantum correlations. Overall, the methods developed in this work enable the integration of SDI technology into portable telecom devices, opening up a new generation of quantum cryptographic hardware.

</details>


### [155] [Quantum Occam Learning: Sample-Supported Expressibility for Circuit-Based Quantum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12211)
*Jeongho Bang,Kyoungho Cho,Jeongwoo Jae*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子数据的奥卡姆理论：对于最多由G个双量子比特门制备的n量子比特纯态，样本复杂度为Θ̃(G/ε²)；对于任意源，提出了近似电路复杂度，并证明了agnostic量子奥卡姆定理——用M份拷贝可学习到最佳G门近似误差加上统计惩罚Õ(√(G/M))；通过自适应模型选择定理去除对G的先验知识，并给出匹配下界，建立了样本支持的表达性定律：在迹距离精度ε下，M个样本最多支持G_supported ≃ Mε²个门。电路复杂度从静态承诺变为自适应统计资源，成为量子机器学习的模型选择原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中，ansatz的表达性只有能在有限拷贝下被学习才具有统计意义。本文旨在建立量子数据的信息论奥卡姆理论，将电路复杂度转化为可学习的统计资源，解决如何从有限量子样本中自适应地确定合适电路复杂度的问题。

Method: 采用度量熵论证分析可学习样本复杂度；定义最佳G门近似误差d_G(ρ̂)和近似电路复杂度C_η(ρ̂)；通过agnostic量子奥卡姆定理给出有限样本下的泛化保证；利用自适应模型选择定理（oracle不等式）消除对G的先验依赖；推导匹配下界建立样本支持的表达性定律。

Result: 电路受限体制下样本复杂度为Θ̃(G/ε²)；agnostic学习误差为d_G(ρ̂)+Õ(√(G/M))；自适应模型选择无需预先知道G；下界表明M个样本在精度ε下最多支持G_supported ≃ Mε²个门（对数因子及2ⁿ断层析饱和限制内）。

Conclusion: 电路复杂度可作为自适应统计资源，而非静态先验假设；该框架将有限电路复杂度转化为量子机器学习的模型选择原则，为量子数据的学习理论提供了信息论基础。

Abstract: A central principle in quantum machine learning is that an ansatz should be expressive enough to represent the quantum data of interest. Yet, the expressibility is statistically meaningful only insofar as it can be learned from finitely many copies of an unknown quantum state. In this work, we develop an information-theoretic Occam theory for quantum data generated by finite-size quantum circuits. For the class $S_{n,G}$ of $n$-qubit pure states preparable with at most $G$ two-qubit gates, a metric-entropy argument gives the realizable sample law $\widetildeΘ(G/ε^2)$ in the circuit-limited regime. For an arbitrary source $\hatρ$, we introduce the best $G$-gate approximation error $d_G(\hatρ)$ and the approximate circuit complexity $C_η(\hatρ)$. We prove an agnostic quantum Occam theorem: with $M$ copies, one can learn up to the best $G$-gate approximation error plus a statistical penalty $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{G/M})$. We then remove the need to know $G$ in advance through an adaptive model-selection theorem whose oracle inequality selects the circuit complexity justified by the data. Matching lower bounds yield a sample-supported expressibility law: at trace-distance accuracy $ε$, $M$ samples can support only $G_{\rm supported} \simeq Mε^2$ gates, up to logarithmic factors and tomography saturation at $2^n$. Thus, the circuit complexity becomes an adaptive statistical resource rather than a static promise. Our framework turns bounded circuit complexity into a model-selection principle for quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [156] [Time-Frequency Grid States for Reconstruction and Correction of Channel-Induced Distortion in Entangled Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12216)
*Siang-Yun Liu,Bo-Ren Huang,Zhi-Xuan Zen,Yen-Hung Chen,Pin-Ju Tsai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验演示了一种利用时频网格态作为内禀频率域参考，结合高斯过程回归模型对非线性坐标变形进行无模型校正的框架，成功将独立纠缠测试态的保真度从76.2%提升至90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时频量子态的可靠重建需要准确的联合谱强度分布，但未知的传输或测量通道畸变会导致重建结果失真，亟需一种无需先验物理模型的通用校正方法。

Method: 利用时频网格态作为内禀频率域参考，通过分析网格点位移，采用高斯过程回归模型构建非线性坐标变形的校正映射，无需假设畸变的先验物理模型。

Result: 时频网格态残余坐标偏差降低约11倍；独立频率纠缠测试态的高斯形状保真度从76.2%提升至90.0%。

Conclusion: 时频网格态可作为实用的计量学资源用于诊断和校正时频量子系统中的畸变，为抗畸变量子通信和高维量子信息处理提供可行路径。

Abstract: Characterization of time-frequency (TF) quantum states requires reliable reconstruction of their TF distributions. However, imperfect transmission or measurement channels can distort reconstructed joint spectral intensities (JSIs), especially when the underlying perturbation mechanism is unknown. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a reconstruction and correction framework that uses a TF grid state as an intrinsic frequency-domain reference. By analyzing the displacement of the grid points, a Gaussian process regression model is employed to reconstruct a correction mapping for the nonlinear coordinate deformation without assuming a prior physical model of the distortion. The learned mapping reduces the residual coordinate deviation of the TF grid state by approximately a factor of 11 and, when applied to an independent frequency-entangled test state, improves the Gaussian-shape fidelity from 76.2\% to 90.0\%. These results establish TF grid states as practical metrological resources for diagnosing and correcting distortions in TF quantum systems, providing a pathway toward distortion-resilient quantum communication and high-dimensional quantum information processing.

</details>


### [157] [Multipartite reference-frame-independent quantum cryptographic communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12284)
*Donghwa Lee,Kyujin Shin,Hyang-Tag Lim,Yosep Kim,Yong-Su Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验上研究了基于GHZ态的多方参考帧无关（RFI）量子密码通信，将双方RFI安全参数C推广到N方参数C_N，推导了仅依赖实验可观测量的渐近密钥率公式，并在全局和局域退极化噪声模型下分析了密钥率随参与者数量N的变化规律。此外，通过四光子GHZ态实现了四方RFI量子密码通信的原理性实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考帧失配会给量子密码协议引入错误，随着参与者数量增加，对齐参考帧变得愈发困难，这严重限制了多方量子密码协议的实际实现。因此，需要一种无需精确参考帧对齐的多方量子密码通信方案。

Method: 将双方RFI安全参数C推广至N方参数C_N，推导出仅由实验可测量量表达的渐近密钥率公式；在全局和局域退极化噪声模型下分析密钥率性能；利用四光子GHZ态进行四方RFI量子密码通信的原理性实验验证。

Result: 增加参与者数量N可增强对全局退极化噪声的鲁棒性，但同时增加了对局域信道噪声的脆弱性；四光子GHZ态实验证实了C_4参数和密钥率在不同参考帧旋转下保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作为大规模多方量子密码通信提供了无需精确参考帧对齐的可行方案，对推动量子网络的实用化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Reference frame mismatch among communication parties introduces errors in quantum cryptographic protocols. As the number of participants increases, aligning reference frames becomes increasingly difficult, complicating multipartite quantum cryptographic implementations. Here, we theoretically and experimentally investigate multipartite reference-frame-independent (RFI) quantum cryptographic communication using Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states. We generalize the bipartite RFI security parameter $C$ to an $N$-party parameter $C_N$ and derive the asymptotic secret key rate expressed solely in terms of experimentally accessible quantities. We analyze the key rate under global and local depolarizing noise models and find that increasing the number of parties $N$ enhances robustness against global depolarizing noise while increasing vulnerability to local channel noise. We also present a proof-of-principle experimental demonstration of four-party RFI quantum cryptographic communication using four-photon GHZ states, confirming the reference-frame invariance of both the $C_4$ parameter and the secret key rate under various reference frame rotations.

</details>


### [158] [An iterative Ising decoder for quantum error correction codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12301)
*Yuanqi Liu,Weilei Zeng,Peixiang Li,Yantong Liu,Guangyao Huang,Yingwen Liu,Dongyang Wang,Junjie Wu,Lingling Lao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: ILOD算法通过交替求解X和Z子哈密顿量并利用贝叶斯先验近似交叉相关性，将量子纠错解码问题中的相互作用体数减半，在保持接近理论阈值的同时显著提升求解器收敛性和运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子纠错中，Ising框架下的精确联合哈密顿量包含高达8体（toric码）或10体（6.6.6色码）的高阶相互作用项，这些项导致求解器收敛性差、运行时间增加，且在嵌入原生2体Ising硬件时产生大量辅助自旋开销。

Method: 提出迭代低阶解码（ILOD）算法，在X和Z子哈密顿量之间交替迭代，通过贝叶斯先验利用另一类型的推断错误配置来重新加权当前类型的耦合，从而近似交叉类型相关性。

Result: 最大相互作用体数减半；toric码阈值4.73%（联合公式4.83%），经验运行时间比随距离d呈(0.81)^d缩放；6.6.6色码在小距离下阈值统计一致，大距离下ILOD保持收敛而联合公式失败。

Conclusion: ILOD在保持解码性能的同时，有效降低了高阶相互作用项带来的计算复杂度和硬件开销，是实际量子纠错解码中可行的优化方案。

Abstract: The Ising framework maps the decoding problem in quantum error correction onto ground-state optimization of a classical Hamiltonian, in which $X$-$Z$ error correlations enter as cross terms. Under phenomenological depolarizing noise, the exact joint formulation contains up to 8-body interactions for the toric code and 10-body for the $6.6.6$ color code. These high-order terms degrade solver convergence, inflate runtime, and raise the auxiliary spin overhead when embedding into native 2-body Ising hardware. In this work, we propose the iterative low-order decoding (ILOD) algorithm, which alternates between $X$- and $Z$-type sub-Hamiltonians, approximating cross-type correlations through Bayesian priors that reweight each type's couplings using the other type's inferred error configuration. This halves the maximum body count of interaction terms in the Hamiltonian, accelerating the solver, restoring convergence at larger code distances, and reducing the total spin count for 2-body embedding by a factor of $2.5$. For the toric code, ILOD attains a threshold of $4.73%$ versus $4.83%$ for the joint formulation, with the empirical runtime ratio scaling as $(0.81)^d$. For the $6.6.6$ color code, their thresholds agree within statistical uncertainty for small code distances, and ILOD remains convergent for larger distances where the joint formulation fails to converge despite a larger annealing budget.

</details>


### [159] [Super-Link Fragility in Asymmetric W-Class States under Quantum Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12307)
*Sougata Bhattacharyya,Fatih Ozaydin,Sovik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称三量子比特W类态$|\overline{W_3^L}\rangle$在标准噪声模型下的纠缠动力学。研究发现，在没有噪声时，纠缠层次为$C_{VB} > C_W > C_{BB}$；在相位阻尼下该层次保持不变；在振幅阻尼下层次发生重排，对称|W⟩态最为鲁棒，而基-基纠缠在有限阈值处消失，称为"超级链接脆弱效应"；在退极化下非对称优势消失。表明W类资源的纠缠鲁棒性由纠缠网络几何、激发扇区和噪声对称性的联合结构共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非对称三量子比特W类态中"超级链接"（更强的顶点-基链接）是否在量子网络任务中具有优势，以及这种非对称纠缠网络几何结构在不同噪声环境下的纠缠鲁棒性表现。

Method: 通过解析比较对称|W⟩态与非对称$|\overline{W_3^L}\rangle$态在四种噪声模型（无噪声、相位阻尼、振幅阻尼、退极化）下的两体并发度动力学。

Result: 无噪声时层次为$C_{VB} > C_W > C_{BB}$；相位阻尼下层次不变且无纠缠猝死；振幅阻尼下层次重排，对称|W⟩态最鲁棒，$C_{BB}$在有限参数阈值处消失（超级链接脆弱效应）；退极化下非对称优势消失，$C_W$与$C_{VB}$具有相同猝死阈值，$C_{BB}$更早消失。

Conclusion: W类资源的纠缠鲁棒性并非由初始并发度单独决定，而是由纠缠网络几何结构、激发扇区和噪声对称性的联合结构共同控制。更强的链接在能量耗散噪声下反而更脆弱。

Abstract: The asymmetric three-qubit W-class state $|\overline{W_3^L}\rangle$ defines an isosceles entanglement-network geometry, (a) two vertex-base (VB) links form stronger bipartite connections, (b) while the base-base (BB) link is weaker. This suggests that concentrating entanglement into a super-link may be advantageous for quantum-network tasks. Here, we show that this intuition is incomplete. We analytically compare the bipartite concurrence dynamics of the symmetric |W> state and the asymmetric $|\overline{W_3^L}\rangle$ state, which differ both in entanglement-network geometry and excitation sector under standard noise models. In the absence of noise, the concurrence hierarchy is C_{VB} > C_W > C_{BB}$. Under phase damping, this hierarchy is preserved for all noise strengths and no entanglement sudden death occurs. Under amplitude damping, however, the hierarchy is reordered. The symmetric |W> state becomes the most robust, while the base-base concurrence of $|\overline{W_3^L}\rangle$ vanishes at the finite threshold of parameter $γ$. We term this reordering as the \textit{Super-Link Fragility Effect}. The same structural asymmetry that produces a stronger vertex-base link also makes it more vulnerable to energy dissipation when coupled with multi-excitation amplitudes. Under depolarization, the asymmetry advantage is erased, with $C_W$ and $C_{VB}$ sharing the same sudden-death threshold for some value of the parameter p, while $C_{BB}$ disappears earlier at some other value of the parameter p. The generalized amplitude damping channel continuously connects the damping-dominated regime to the pure-excitation limit, where the initial hierarchy is restored. These results show that entanglement robustness in $W$-class resources is controlled not by initial concurrence alone, but by the joint structure of entanglement-network geometry, excitation sector, and noise symmetry.

</details>


### [160] [Partitioned Iterative Quantum Scheduling of Satellites for Urgent Disaster Response: Case study of Wildfire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12310)
*Lucas T. Braydwood,Taejin Park,Hirofumi Hashimoto,Zoe Gonzalez Izquierdo,Andrew Michaelis,Eleanor Rieffel,Shon Grabbe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以野火检测为背景，研究量子算法在卫星调度问题中的应用，提出了结合迭代量子算法框架和分布式量子计算方法的并行化方案，并在真实数据集上验证了其可行性，尽管当前量子子过程规模较小尚未体现显著量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着星座规模日益庞大，卫星调度面临严峻计算挑战，而野火检测等近实时地球观测任务对调度效率要求极高，亟需探索新型计算范式（如量子计算）来解决这一难题。

Method: 结合迭代量子算法框架（具有解析性保证）与分布式量子计算方法，设计分布式/并行化方案与量子算法协同，并在真实野火检测数据集上进行验证。

Result: 当前量子子过程规模较小，尚未展现出显著量子优势，但验证了所提技术的实用性。

Conclusion: 研究成果为分布式量子计算的发展铺平了道路，证明了量子算法在卫星调度等实际问题中的潜力。

Abstract: The standard in Earth-observation tasks today is having near real-time access to surface images in response to changing conditions. For instance, as urban environments interface more with wildlands and wildfires become less predictable, their tracking with satellite resources becomes essential. This requires the coordination of increasingly large constellations of satellites, giving rise to challenging computational problems. With wildfire detection and tracking as a backdrop, we investigate the power of special purpose and novel computing paradigms to tackle the ensuing satellite scheduling problems, making a compelling case for quantum algorithms. We bring quantum scheduling algorithms closer to implementation by examining both the emerging iterative quantum algorithm framework, which comes with analytic guarantees compared to some classical algorithms, and distributed quantum computing methods whose relevance is on the rise as utility-scale problems begin to get solved with quantum computers. Drawing strength from several computing fronts, we develop a distributed/parallelization scheme in conjunction with the quantum algorithm design and apply these techniques to real-world datasets for wildfire detection. While our quantum subprocesses are currently too small to see significant quantum advantage, our results validate the utility of these techniques, and continue forging the path toward distributed quantum computing.

</details>


### [161] [Quantum repeater segment with free-space coupled co-trapped ions using telecom photon interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12313)
*Max Bergerhoff,Pascal Baumgart,Christian Haen,Jonas Meiers,Tobias Bauer,Jonas Haferkamp,Christoph Becher,Jürgen Eschner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了基于两个共捕获自由空间耦合的40Ca+离子的量子中继器段，通过将光子转换为电信C波段并在440米光纤（每臂220米）传输后进行贝尔测量，实现了存储器间纠缠，保真度≥68(8)%，证明了40Ca+离子作为量子中继器硬件平台的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继器段是连接量子中继器单元和量子计算机的基本构建模块，本文旨在实验实现一个基于 trapped 离子的量子中继器段，以验证该平台的实际可行性。

Method: 利用两个共捕获的自由空间耦合40Ca+离子发射光子，将光子转换为电信C波段，经过440米光纤（每臂220米）传输后进行光子贝尔态测量，从而在量子存储器之间创建纠缠。

Result: 成功生成了纠缠的|Ψ+⟩贝尔态，保真度≥68(8)%。

Conclusion: 实验结果突出了捕获的40Ca+离子作为有前景的量子中继器硬件平台。

Abstract: A quantum repeater segment is a basic building block of a quantum repeater, generating buffered entanglement of quantum memories to connect quantum repeater cells. It also enables the connection between quantum computers. In the implementation we present here, photons emitted from two co-trapped free-space coupled $^{40}$Ca$^+$ ions are converted to the telecom-C band and interfered after transmission over 440$\,$m of optical fiber (220$\,$m per arm), where a photonic Bell measurement is performed to create entanglement between the memories. With this scheme we generate an entangled $\left|Ψ^+\right\rangle$ Bell state with $\ge 68(8)\,$% fidelity, highlighting trapped $^{40}$Ca$^+$ ions as a promising quantum repeater hardware platform.

</details>


### [162] [Fermions are fundamentally more nonlocal than Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12363)
*Fatemeh Moradi Kalarde,Sadra Boreiri,Xiangling Xu,Lucas Tendick,Salman Beigi,Paolo Perinotti,Tommaso Guaita,Marc-Olivier Renou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，不可区分的费米子（费米子信息载体，即febits）在量子网络中能够产生比可区分粒子或玻色子更强的非局域关联，且这种关联无法通过额外通信来模拟。这一结果表明，费米子的不可区分性和反对易关系是量子信息处理中不可或缺的操作性资源，并超越所有基于量子比特（qubits）的分布式计算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子理论内不同统计性质粒子（费米子、玻色子、可区分粒子）是否具有本质上的非局域性差异，并论证费米子的不可区分性和反对易关系如何成为超越量子比特的计算资源。

Method: 类比贝尔定理中的非局域性论证方法，在量子网络场景下比较不可区分费米子、不可区分玻色子与可区分粒子（标准量子比特）之间的关联强度；证明费米子可以产生其他粒子类型无法模拟（无需额外通信）的关联。

Result: 不可区分费米子在量子网络中产生的关联，严格强于可区分粒子或不可区分玻色子所能产生的关联，且这些关联无法通过额外通信来复现。这表明费米子天然具有更强的非局域性资源。

Conclusion: 费米子的反对易关系和不可区分性是量子信息处理中不可回避的资源；febits（费米子信息载体）能够在分布式计算任务中严格超越所有基于qubits的协议，提示我们需要超越qubit框架，发展基于费米子的信息理论。

Abstract: Bell's theorem shows that entangled quantum particles can exhibit correlations that classical particles cannot reproduce without an additional nonlocal resource, such as communication. In this sense, quantum particles are fundamentally more nonlocal than classical ones, and entanglement becomes unavoidable in physics. Here we prove the analogous result within quantum theory itself: indistinguishable fermions transmitted through a quantum network can generate correlations that distinguishable particles or indistinguishable bosons cannot reproduce without additional communication. In the same sense, fermions are fundamentally more nonlocal than bosons or distinguishable particles, motivating fermionic anticommutation and indistinguishability as unavoidable operational resources. Our result further implies that fermions can strictly surpass all qubit-based protocols for certain distributed computing tasks, demonstrating that a complete understanding of information processing requires going beyond qubits to fermionic information carriers - febits.

</details>


### [163] [The Simplified Stabilizer ZX-Calculus is Minimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12383)
*Harry K. Stoltz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了ZX演算的稳定子片段中，红/绿紧致结构重合规则和双代数规则相对于Backens-Perdrix-Wang的连接元规则是单独必要的，从而确认arXiv:1709.08903提出的规则集没有冗余重写规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明稳定子片段的大多数重写规则是必要的，但红/绿紧致结构重合规则和双代数规则的必要性尚未得到证明，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用反模型（countermodel）论证方法，分别证明这两条规则相对于连接元规则是单独必要的。

Result: 两条规则（红/绿紧致结构重合规则和双代数规则）均被证明是必要的，arXiv:1709.08903提出的完整规则集无冗余。

Conclusion: ZX演算稳定子片段的规则集是最小完备的，不存在可移除的冗余规则。

Abstract: The stabilizer fragment of the ZX calculus is amongst the most important fragments of the theory. The closely related Clifford+T fragment is approximately universal (arXiv:1705.11151). Additionally, the stabilizer calculus can be described by a small collection of rewrites, most of which have been shown to be necessary (arXiv:1709.08903). However, two rules, describing the red/green compact-structure coincidence and the important bialgebra law, had not been shown to be necessary. We present a countermodel-style argument showing that both of these rules are individually necessary relative to the connectivity meta-rule of Backens--Perdrix--Wang (arXiv:1709.08903), and hence establish that the rule set presented in arXiv:1709.08903 has no redundant rewrite rule.

</details>


### [164] [Scaling-optimal purification of noisy qubit unitary channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12394)
*Ryotaro Niwa,Satoshi Yoshida,Koki Ono,Takeru Utsumi,Zhaoyi Li,Yuxiang Yang,Ryuji Takagi,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声量子比特酉信道的纯化问题，旨在通过超信道将带 depolarizing 噪声的未知酉信道恢复为原始无噪声酉信道。数值证据表明有限次使用时顺序策略优于并行策略，并提出了基于纠缠辅助量子纠错码的 U(2)-协变并行协议，可实现噪声强度 O(1/n) 的抑制，且在低噪声区域渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带 depolarizing 噪声的未知量子比特酉信道中恢复原始无噪声酉信道，研究噪声纯化的基本限制与最优策略。

Method: 通过数值模拟比较顺序与并行策略的性能差异；提出一种 U(2)-协变的并行协议，基于新型纠缠辅助量子纠错码实现噪声抑制。

Result: 顺序策略在有限次使用时严格优于并行策略；所提并行协议实现噪声强度 O(1/n) 的一阶抑制，且在低噪声区域该标度渐近最优。

Conclusion: 量子信道纯化与态纯化存在本质区别（顺序 vs 并行策略优劣反转）；纠缠辅助纠错码可达到最优的噪声抑制标度。

Abstract: We consider the problem of purifying noisy qubit unitary channels. Given the ability to apply an unknown qubit unitary channel followed by depolarizing noise, we aim to construct a superchannel that purifies the noisy unitary back to the original unknown unitary. We first provide numerical evidence that sequential strategies can strictly outperform parallel strategies when the number of channel uses is finite, highlighting the fundamental distinction from state purification. We then provide a concrete $\mathrm{U}(2)$-covariant parallel protocol based on a novel entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting code that suppresses the first-order noise strength as $O(1/n)$ with $n$ channel uses and show this scaling is asymptotically optimal in the low-noise regime, even when sequential strategies are allowed.

</details>

### [165] [Slowly rotating traversable wormholes supported by radially varying string-fluid matter: From regular geometries to photon trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11261)
*A. Errehymy,B. Turimov,M. A. Khan,S. Usanov,Z. Yasakov,Z. Avezmuratova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由径向变化的弦流体支撑的缓慢旋转可穿越虫洞，发现流体性质从类德西特核心平滑过渡到弦主导区域，产生规则、无视界、渐近平坦的时空结构。旋转引入的参考系拖拽效应会在光子路径上产生细微差异，不同的弦流体径向轮廓会产生独特的光子环模式，为观测虫洞旋转和内部物质分布提供了潜在特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种物理上自洽的、由各向异性弦流体支撑的可穿越虫洞模型，解决虫洞物质来源的物理合理性问题，并研究旋转效应对时空结构和光传播的影响。

Method: 采用径向变化的弦流体（其性质随距喉部距离改变），使横向压力依赖于半径，从而自然适应周围几何，构建缓慢旋转的可穿越虫洞模型，并分析其度规、能量密度、形状函数以及光子轨道的性质。

Result: 径向变化的弦流体能够产生规则、无视界、渐近平坦的时空；旋转产生参考系拖拽效应，使共转与逆转光子轨迹产生细微差异，且效应在喉部附近最强；不同的弦流体径向轮廓产生不同的光子环模式。

Conclusion: 径向变化的弦流体为可穿越虫洞提供了灵活且物理自洽的物质来源，能在真空状区域和弦主导区域之间平滑过渡，各向异性物质同时影响时空曲率和光的传播，为理解无视界奇异时空及其潜在观测效应提供了新框架。

Abstract: This work investigates slowly rotating traversable wormholes supported by string fluids whose properties vary with distance from the throat. This radial variation allows the matter to transition smoothly from a de Sitter-like core near the center to a string-dominated environment further out, producing a regular, horizon-free, and asymptotically flat spacetime. By letting the transverse pressure depend on radius, the fluid naturally adapts to the surrounding geometry, resulting in a well-behaved energy density and shape function. Even modest rotation introduces frame-dragging effects that gently twist photon paths, creating subtle differences between co-rotating and counter-rotating trajectories. These effects are strongest near the throat, while at larger distances the spacetime is largely governed by the static gravitational potentials. Circular photon orbits reveal that the interplay of the redshift function, wormhole shape, and rotation shapes the photon-sphere structure. Different radial profiles of the string fluid generate distinctive photon-ring patterns, offering potential observational signatures of both the rotation and the internal matter distribution. Overall, radially varying string fluids provide a flexible and physically consistent source for traversable wormholes, bridging smoothly between vacuum-like and string-dominated regions while maintaining regularity and supporting slow rotation. This study highlights how anisotropic matter can influence both curvature and light propagation, providing a realistic framework for horizonless exotic spacetimes and suggesting new avenues to explore subtle observational effects around traversable wormholes.

</details>


### [166] [Quasinormal modes and tidal responses of black holes in generic anisotropic matter environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11380)
*Yu-Qian Zhao,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套微扰框架，用于研究球对称黑洞嵌入一般（可能各向异性）物质环境中的情况，推导了包含多项式径向压力的爱因斯坦星团的解析推广，并发现潮汐Love数对物质各向异性高度敏感，可以出现数量级变化、消失甚至负值，而测地线结构和轴向量子模谱主要受整体引力红移效应支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究局限于零径向压力或微扰物质构型，缺乏处理一般各向异性物质环境中黑洞的通用微扰框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立更普适的理论工具。

Method: 基于球对称假设，发展了一套包含各向异性物质（特别是有径向压力）的微扰理论；将爱因斯坦星团推广到包含多项式径向压力的情形；通过分析测地线结构、轴向准正规模和潮汐Love数来表征环境效应。

Result: 1) 测地线结构和轴向准正规模谱主要受整体引力红移效应支配，径向压力系统性增强环境修正；2) 潮汐Love数对各向异性极为敏感，可出现数量级为1的偏差，包括严格静态磁Love数为零或负值；3) 给出了完整的线性化方程组，可用于ringdown分析和极端质量比旋进等扩展研究。

Conclusion: 该微扰框架为研究黑洞在一般各向异性物质环境中的动力学行为提供了有效工具，特别适用于环down分析和极端质量比旋进等天体物理场景。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for a black hole embedded in a generic, possibly anisotropic, matter environment under spherical symmetry. Our approach extends previous analyses restricted to vanishing radial pressure or to perturbative matter configurations. Within this framework, we derive an analytical generalization of the Einstein cluster that incorporates a polytropic radial pressure, and we investigate the properties of this solution. We show that both the geodesic structure and the axial quasinormal-mode spectrum remain predominantly governed by an overall gravitational redshift effect, while the radial pressure systematically enhances the environmental corrections. In contrast, the tidal Love numbers are substantially more sensitive, and can exhibit order-unity deviations, including vanishing and negative strictly static magnetic Love numbers for sufficiently large anisotropy. We present the full linearized equations, which can be applied to various extensions, including ringdown analysis and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals.

</details>


### [167] [Linearized stability of T-duality quantum-inspired thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11413)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过弦论T-对偶性构造了量子修正的薄壳虫洞，发现其在中尺度喉径区域存在无条件稳定性窗口，且无需奇异物质即可满足能量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿越虫洞需要奇异物质且不稳定，本文旨在利用量子引力修正（弦T-对偶）同时解决奇点问题和稳定性问题。

Method: 将两份量子修正正则时空（来自弦T-对偶）沿薄壳粘合，推导表面应力张量，分析能量条件，并进行线性化稳定性分析。

Result: 静态构型中，足够大喉径可满足零能量条件和强能量条件；稳定性分析显示：最小喉径附近不稳定，中间尺度（a~l₀）存在无条件稳定窗口（几何稳定阈值负值），大尺度恢复Schwarzschild行为需刚性物态方程。

Conclusion: T-对偶尺度l₀不仅是正则化参数，更是打开经典虫洞所不具备的无条件稳定区域的关键物理参数，表明量子引力修正可同时消除奇点并赋予虫洞动力学可行性。

Abstract: Wormholes that are traversable in principle offer fascinating insights into general relativity, yet they typically require exotic matter and suffer from stability issues. We construct a thin-shell wormhole by gluing two copies of a quantum-corrected, regular spacetime obtained from string T-duality. This regularisation replaces the classical curvature singularity with a smooth core and introduces a fundamental length scale $l_0$. For the static configuration, we derive the surface stresses and show that, unlike the Schwarzschild case, the null and strong energy conditions can be satisfied for sufficiently large throat radii. A linearised stability analysis reveals a rich landscape: close to the minimum allowed throat radius the configuration is unstable; at intermediate radii ($a \sim l_0$) the geometric stability threshold becomes negative, yielding a window of \emph{unconditional stability} where any convex surface mass function suffices; at large radii the wormhole recovers Schwarzschild-like behaviour and stability requires a stiff equation of state. The T-duality scale $l_0$ is thus not merely a regulariser but a key physical parameter that opens a novel region of unconditional stability absent in classical thin-shell wormholes. Our results suggest that quantum-gravity-motivated modifications can simultaneously cure singularities and make traversable wormholes dynamically viable, providing new targets for gravitational-wave astronomy and theoretical studies of exotic compact objects.

</details>


### [168] [On phase-space singular surfaces in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11453)
*Dražen Glavan,David M. J. Vokrouhlický*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对Jordan框架下的度规f(R)引力进行了哈密顿约束分析，发现在f'(R)=0和f''(R)=0的奇异相空间曲面上正则约束分类退化。对于满足f(R)=0和f'(R)=0的精确背景，线性化谱为空。Starobinsky模型中的FLRW轨迹可以穿越f'(R)=0曲面，但非均匀扰动在穿越时会出现退化的约束结构。区别了完全位于奇异曲面上的背景与动态穿越奇异曲面的背景，二者导致不同的扰动退化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究f(R)引力中奇异相空间曲面的约束分类退化及其对线性化扰动谱的影响，澄清完全位于奇异曲面与动态穿越奇异曲面的背景之间的物理区别。

Method: 采用哈密顿约束分析方法分析f(R)引力的正则约束结构，结合线性化扰动理论考察奇异曲面上的谱行为，并在Starobinsky模型中研究FLRW轨迹穿越f'(R)=0曲面的动力学过程。

Result: 发现f'(R)=0和f''(R)=0曲面导致约束分类退化；满足f(R)=0和f'(R)=0的精确背景线性化谱为空；Starobinsky模型的FLRW轨迹可穿越f'(R)=0曲面但扰动约束结构退化；穿越条件更应理解为扰动演化的正则性条件而非Dirac-Bergmann算法中的普通约束。

Conclusion: 完全位于奇异曲面上的背景与动态穿越奇异曲面的背景导致不同的扰动退化行为，为理解f(R)引力中奇异相空间几何的物理意义提供了新视角。

Abstract: We perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis of metric $f(R)$ gravity in the Jordan frame and show that the regular constraint classification degenerates on singular phase-space surfaces located at $f'(R)\!=\!0$ and $f''(R)\!=\!0$. We then study the perturbative implications of these surfaces. For exact backgrounds satisfying $f(R)\!=\!0$ and $f'(R)\!=\!0$, the linearized spectrum is empty; the known pure $R^2$ result is therefore a special case of a more general degeneracy in $f(R)$ gravity. We also show that FLRW trajectories in the Starobinsky model can cross the surface $f'(R)=0$, but that inhomogeneous perturbations develop a degenerate constraint structure at the crossing. The resulting crossing condition is better interpreted as a regularity condition for perturbative evolution than as an ordinary constraint within the Dirac--Bergmann algorithm. Together, these results distinguish backgrounds that lie entirely on a singular surface from backgrounds that cross one dynamically, and show that the two situations lead to different perturbative degeneracies.

</details>


### [169] [Statistical-Uncertainty-Driven Selection of Evaluation Frequency for Time-Dependent Sensing Calibration: A Demonstration with KAGRA Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11504)
*Shingo Hido,Takahiro Yamamoto,Dan Chen,Takahiro Sawada,Shinji Miyoki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种引力波探测器中传感响应校准频率的统计预选框架，并将其应用于KAGRA数据。在腔极点频率约18 Hz而实际校准线注入在32.7 Hz的约束下，该方法在244 Hz处选出最佳评估频率，将振幅区间宽度缩减至参考频率的约四分之一，同时保持相位区间宽度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波应变h(t)的精确校准对探测和天体物理推断均至关重要。实际运行探测器中，传感响应的缓慢时间变化通过校准线跟踪，但实际约束可能阻止在有利于精确估计传感侧参数的频率处注入校准线。因此需要一种在约束条件下预选评估频率的统计框架。

Method: 提出统计预选框架：1）构建每个候选评估频率的传感函数；2）利用样本分布的经验百分位数量化逐段的统计不确定性；3）结合振幅和相位的区间宽度计算评分，对候选频率进行排序。应用于KAGRA在第四轮LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA联合观测运行第一时段的数据，其中腔极点频率约18 Hz，实际传感侧校准线注入在32.7 Hz。

Result: 当振幅区间宽度1%和相位区间宽度1度权重相等时，在所有4096秒分析段中均选出244 Hz作为最优评估频率。相对32.7 Hz参考频率，振幅区间宽度在宽频率范围内缩减至约四分之一，相位区间宽度总体相当。此外还单独评估了频率平移引入的偏差。

Conclusion: 所提方法为实际操作约束下评估频率的统计预选提供了有用框架，能有效改善传感参数估计精度。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of the gravitational-wave strain h(t) is essential for both detection and astrophysical inference. In operating detectors, slow temporal variations in the sensing response are tracked using calibration lines, but practical constraints can prevent those lines from being injected at frequencies that are favorable for precise estimation of sensing-side parameters. We present a statistical framework for preselecting evaluation frequencies under such constraints. We apply this framework to KAGRA data from the first part of the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Observing Run, for which the nominal cavity-pole frequency was about 18 Hz, while the sensing-side calibration line used in practice was injected at 32.7 Hz. For each candidate evaluation frequency, we construct the sensing function, quantify its segment-wise statistical uncertainty from empirical percentiles of the sample distribution, and rank the candidates using a score that combines the interval widths of the amplitude and phase. When a 1% amplitude interval width and a 1 degree phase interval width are weighted equally, 244 Hz is selected in all 4096 s analysis segments throughout the analyzed period. Relative to the reference frequency of 32.7 Hz, the amplitude interval width is reduced to about one quarter over a broad frequency range, while the phase interval width remains broadly comparable. We also assess the discrepancy introduced by frequency translation separately. These results suggest that the proposed method provides a useful statistical preselection framework for evaluation frequencies under practical operational constraints.

</details>


### [170] [Horizon absorption in eccentric precessing binary black hole inspirals and its importance for gravitational wave data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11705)
*Alberto Álvaro-Díaz,Gonzalo Morras*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次在领头阶后牛顿展开中推导了黑洞视界吸收对双黑洞并合的影响，并纳入偏心轨道和自旋进动效应，发现偏心双星中该效应可被测量，而准圆双星中则难以探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞演化过程中，视界吸收会交换轨道与黑洞之间的能量和角动量，影响引力波信号，准确建模对当前和未来探测器的信号分析至关重要，同时可检验致密天体的性质。

Method: 在领头阶后牛顿框架下，首次推导了包含轨道偏心率和自旋诱导进动效应的视界吸收修正，并将这些修正纳入 pyEFPEHM 波形模型，通过轨道去相分析、波形失配和贝叶斯参数估计量化其影响。

Result: 效应在自旋与轨道角动量（反）对齐（|χ·l̂|~1）、高度不对称质量比（q≪1）、长频率跨度（log(f_max/f_min)≫1）的系统中最大。忽略该效应会中等信噪比下导致参数估计偏差。准圆双星中偏差被吸收而难以探测，偏心双星中丰富的信号形态打破简并，使视界吸收可在高信噪比事件中被测量。

Conclusion: 偏心双星系统为探测黑洞视界吸收效应提供了独特窗口，而准圆双星中该效应难以与其它参数解耦，对引力波精确建模和黑洞本质检验有重要意义。

Abstract: During the evolution of a binary black hole, energy and angular momentum are exchanged between the orbital motion and the individual black holes through horizon absorption, modifying both the binary dynamics and the black hole masses and spins. This leaves an imprint on the emitted gravitational waves that may be relevant for the accurate modeling of signals observed by current and future detectors, while also offering a probe of the nature of compact objects. In this work, we derive, for the first time and at leading order in the post-Newtonian expansion, the effect of horizon absorption in binary black hole inspirals with both orbital eccentricity and spin-induced precession, and we incorporate these corrections into the pyEFPEHM waveform model. We then quantify their impact through analytical estimates of the orbital dephasing, waveform mismatches, and Bayesian parameter-estimation studies. The effect is largest for systems with large spin components (anti-)aligned with the orbital angular momentum ($|\vecχ_i \cdot \hat{l}| \sim 1$), highly unequal mass ratios ($q=m_2/m_1 \ll 1$), and long inspirals spanning a wide frequency range ($\log(f_\mathrm{max}/f_\mathrm{min}) \gg 1$). For such systems, neglecting horizon absorption biases the recovered binary parameters at moderate signal-to-noise ratios. In quasi-circular binaries these biases largely absorb the effect, rendering it difficult to detect. In eccentric binaries, however, the richer signal morphology breaks this degeneracy, making horizon absorption potentially measurable in high signal-to-noise-ratio events.

</details>


### [171] [Periodic orbits as probes of charged loop quantum gravity black holes through gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11728)
*Abolhassan Mohammadi,Arun Kumar,Hongwei Tan,Sushant G. Ghosh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了环圈量子引力（LQG）修正的带电黑洞时空中极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统的引力波发射，通过分析测试粒子运动、周期轨道分类及引力波形，发现其特征应变峰值位于毫赫兹频段，可被LISA、Taiji和TianQin等探测器观测，从而对LQG聚合参数施加强场约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进（EMRI）产生的引力波可直接探测黑洞的强场几何结构，而环圈量子引力（LQG）对经典黑洞奇点的修正为检验量子引力效应提供了独特窗口。本文旨在研究LQG聚合效应对EMRI动力学及引力波信号的影响，探索未来空间引力波探测器约束量子引力参数的可能性。

Method: 采用LQG聚合参数δ_b由质量M和电荷Q唯一确定的带电黑洞时空，通过构建有效势确定最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）和边际束缚轨道（MBO）；利用Levin-Perez-Giz zoom-whirl分类法以三重整数(z,w,v)标记周期轨道，通过有理频率比q=ω_φ/ω_r-1定位轨道；在四极矩近似下估计EMRI引力波形，获取时域和频域极化信号。

Result: 时域极化信号呈现zoom-whirl形态，波形振幅和相位依赖于LQG参数；特征应变峰值位于毫赫兹频段，且对所有电荷参数Q均超出LISA、Taiji和TianQin的预期灵敏度。

Conclusion: 未来空间引力波观测可对LQG聚合参数在强场区域施加有意义的约束。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRI) provide a direct probe of the strong-field geometry of black holes. Motivated by this, we study the motion of test particles and the resulting gravitational wave emission in the spacetime of a charged black hole inspired by loop quantum gravity (LQG), where the classical singularity is replaced by a smooth transition surface arising from the LQG polymerization, in which its radius is set by the LQG area gap condition. As a result, the polymerization parameter $δ_b$ is uniquely determined by the mass $M$ and charge parameter $Q$, so that all cases examined in this work contain LQG correction. By constructing the effective potential, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the marginally bound orbit (MBO) are determined. Periodic orbits are classified using the Levin-Perez-Giz zoom-whirl taxonomy, showing how the orbit topology shapes the waveform, so that each closed trajectory is labeled by the triple integer $(z, w, v)$ and located through the rational frequency ratio $q = ω_φ/ω_r - 1$. Within the quadrupole approximation, the gravitational waveforms for an EMRIs are estimated, and the resulting polarizations are obtained in the time-domain and frequency-domain. The resulting polarizations in the time-domain exhibit a zoom-whirl morphology, with the waveform amplitude and phase dependent on the LQG parameter. The characteristic strain peaks in the millihertz band for all values of the charge parameter $Q$, and they exceed the projected sensitivities of LISA, Taiji, and TianQin, suggesting that future observations could place meaningful constraints on the LQG polymerization parameter in the strong-field regime.

</details>


### [172] [Comisso-Asenjo Mechanism in Rotating $\mathcal{N}=2,U(1)^2$ Gauged Supergravity Black Holes: Extended Comparison With Kerr Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11732)
*Abhinav Jaguri,Hemwati Nandan,Pankaj Sheoran,Sanjar Shaymatov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合$\mathcal{N}=2,\,U(1)^2$规范超引力黑洞附近通过Comisso-Asenjo磁重联过程提取能量的机制，分析了独立参数集$(N_g,g,v,e)$与自旋参数$a$对提取能量、效率和功率的影响，发现某些低自旋($a\sim0.39$)情况下的能量提取效率可超过极端Kerr黑洞($a\sim1$)，且可超越极端Kerr效率极限($\eta>1.495$)。同时研究了取向角$\xi$和磁化参数$\sigma_0$的影响，并发现旋转黑洞时空中的可观测Lundquist数$S_{\rm obs}$通过lapse函数$\alpha$获得观测者依赖的角度依赖性，导致偏离标准Sweet-Parker标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耦合规范超引力黑洞附近通过CA磁重联过程提取能量的机制，特别是研究独立参数集与自旋参数对能量提取的综合影响，寻找在较低自旋情况下实现高效率能量提取的最优参数组合，并扩展与Kerr黑洞的比较。

Method: 采用CA磁重联过程模型，通过独立参数集$(N_g,g,v,e)$和自旋参数$a$进行参数扫描，区分低、中、高和混合四类参数组合，仅研究极端情况；使用Kendall's Tau统计方法识别关键参数的促进或抑制作用，分析可观测Lundquist数在旋转黑洞时空中的行为。

Result: 发现低自旋($a\sim0.39$)情况下可实现比Kerr极端情况更高的能量提取效率；极端Kerr效率极限($\eta>1.495$)可被超越；通过Kendall's Tau识别了关键独立参数作为能量提取过程中的促进或抑制因素；发现可观测Lundquist数$S_{\rm obs}$通过lapse函数$\alpha$获得观测者依赖的角度依赖性，偏离标准Sweet-Parker标度律。

Conclusion: 耦合规范超引力黑洞的CA磁重联能量提取在较低自旋下即可达到甚至超过极端Kerr黑洞的效率，为黑洞能量提取提供了更高效的参数空间；可观测磁重联速率受观测者位置影响，需在实测中考虑lapse函数修正。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate energy extraction via the Comisso-Asenjo (CA) magnetic reconnection process near a coupled $\mathcal{N}=2,\,U(1)^2$ gauged supergravity Black Hole (BH). Our study focuses on the combined impact of the independent parameter set $p_i\in(N_g,g,v,e)$ with the spin parameter $a$ on the extracted energy ($ε_{\pm}$), efficiency ($η$), and extracted power ($\mathcal{P}_{CA}$), aiming to identify optimal combinations where energy can be extracted with higher efficiency in certain cases at lower spin $(a\sim0.39)$ than the Kerr extremal case $(a\sim1)$. Using the spacetime parameters, we explore various cases leading to distinct spacetimes and provide an extended comparison with the Kerr Black Hole (KBH). We also examine the influence of the orientation angle ($ξ$) and magnetization parameter ($σ_0$) on both efficiency and extracted power. Investigating low $[\,\forall p_i<0.2 \land N_g<0.08\,]$, mid $[\,\exists p_i\ge0.5 \land N_g\in(0.08,0.15)\,]$, high $[\,\exists p_i>0.7 \land N_g\in(0.16,0.23)\,]$, and mixed $[\,\forall p_i\in(0,1) \land N_g\in(0,0.23)\,]$ parameter combinations, we explore only extremal cases for all spacetime parameters and demonstrate that the extremal Kerr efficiency limit ($η>1.495$) can be exceeded. The statistical Kendall's Tau approach allows us to identify the key independent parameters acting as boosters or dampers in the energy extraction process and to visualize the relationship between $(N_g,g,v,e)$ and the physical outputs $(a_{\rm ext},r_E,r_{\rm ergo},ε_{\pm},η,\mathcal{P}_{CA},R_η,R_{\mathcal{P}})$. Furthermore, we show that the observable Lundquist number $S_{\rm obs}$ in rotating BH spacetimes acquires an observer-dependent angular dependence through the lapse function $(α)$. This leads to deviations from the standard Sweet-Parker scaling when expressed in terms of observable quantities.

</details>


### [173] [Critical Coupling Surfaces in $κ(R,T)$ Gravity: Regularity, Gravitational Screening, and Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11942)
*Ginés R. Pérez Teruel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了κ(R,T)引力理论中临界耦合条件κ=0的数学结构和物理意义，证明该条件不会导致理论奇异性，并揭示了其作为引力屏蔽面分离吸引与排斥相、阻碍全局爱因斯坦框架描述等关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多假设有效引力耦合非零，但临界超曲面（κ=0）是许多允许耦合函数的一般特征，其物理含义和数学性质尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过分析κ(R,T)引力中守恒律的重写形式，证明非守恒方程的表观奇异性是人为产物；推导临界超曲面的相容性条件(∇^μκ)T_{μν}=0，并探讨其几何与物理含义。

Result: κ=0处的基本方程保持正则；导出临界超曲面的相容性条件；揭示了临界耦合超曲面阻碍全局爱因斯坦框架转换的特性。

Conclusion: 临界耦合超曲面是κ(R,T)引力中分离吸引与排斥引力相的重要结构，其存在使该理论区别于仅基于能量-动量张量代数重定义的理论，具有潜在的宇宙学和天体物理后果。

Abstract: We investigate the critical regime $κ(R,T)=0$ in $κ(R,T)$ gravity. While most studies assume a non-vanishing effective gravitational coupling, the existence of critical hypersurfaces where $κ$ vanishes is a generic feature of many admissible coupling functions. We show that the apparent singularity of the non-conservation equation is an artifact of a rewritten form of the conservation law and that the fundamental equations remain regular at $κ=0$. We further analyze the structure of critical hypersurfaces, derive the associated compatibility condition $(\nabla^μκ)T_{μν}=0$, and discuss their interpretation as gravitational screening surfaces separating attractive and repulsive gravitational phases. The existence of critical coupling hypersurfaces also obstructs a global Einstein-frame description, distinguishing $κ(R,T)$ gravity from theories based solely on algebraic redefinitions of the energy-momentum tensor. Possible cosmological and astrophysical consequences are briefly explored.

</details>


### [174] [A post-selected quantum model of cosmic acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12297)
*Dimitris Lionas,Charis Anastopoulos,Konstantinos Gourgouliatos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子后选择的宇宙学模型，通过后选择和粗粒化在晚期产生有效加速膨胀，无需引入宇宙学常数或暗能量，并与超新星和宇宙计时器数据形成竞争性拟合，同时自然避免巧合问题，预测的jerk参数显著区别于ΛCDM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙加速膨胀的起源是宇宙学的核心问题，现有解释（宇宙学常数、动力学暗能量、修改引力）均有局限性。作者希望探索一种源于量子理论基本特征——后选择——的替代机制，而不引入额外的宇宙学流体或修改引力理论。

Method: 基于之前关于后选择准经典动力学的研究，构建最小预测量子宇宙学模型：在标准弗里德曼演化基础上引入后选择和粗粒化，使加速膨胀作为宏观量子宇宙学效应自然涌现，模型最多包含两个额外参数。

Result: 与Ia型超新星和宇宙计时器数据的拟合在统计上与ΛCDM模型具有竞争力；自然避免巧合问题；早期宇宙恢复标准的辐射主导和物质主导行为；预测的今天jerk参数显著不同于ΛCDM的预测值。

Conclusion: 宇宙加速膨胀可能源于宏观量子宇宙学效应，而非额外的宇宙学流体或修改引力；该模型为解释宇宙加速提供了一种概念上全新的路径。

Abstract: The origin of cosmic acceleration remains a central problem in cosmology, commonly attributed to a cosmological constant within the $Λ$CDM model or to dynamical dark energy. Here, we develop an alternative approach in which acceleration emerges from quantum post-selection, a standard feature of quantum theory that is not usually incorporated into cosmological modelling. While quantum theory admits both pre-selected and post-selected ensembles, quantum cosmological models are almost exclusively formulated in terms of initial conditions. Building on previous work on post-selected quasiclassical dynamics, we construct a minimal predictive cosmological model in which post-selection and coarse-graining generate effective late-time acceleration without introducing a cosmological constant, dark energy, or modifications of general relativity. The resulting expansion history is highly constrained theoretically and depends on at most two parameters beyond standard Friedmann evolution. Confrontation with type Ia supernova and cosmic chronometer data yields statistically competitive fits while naturally avoiding the coincidence problem. The model also reproduces the standard radiation- and matter-dominated behaviour at early times and predicts a present-day jerk parameter significantly different from the $Λ$CDM value. These results suggest that cosmic acceleration may arise as a macroscopic quantum cosmological effect rather than from additional cosmological fluids or modified gravitational dynamics.

</details>
